# The Moonlight of the Ascendant
<note>original: "Lagnachandrika." In Vedic astrology, the "Lagna" or ascendant is the zodiac sign rising on the eastern horizon at the time of birth. "Chandrika" means moonlight, metaphorically referring to a text that illuminates or clarifies a subject.</note>

## Accompanied by the 'Easy Clarifier' Hindi Commentary
<note>The 'Subodhini' (meaning 'easy to understand') is a popular explanatory commentary that translates and interprets the original Sanskrit verses into Hindi for a modern audience.</note>

---

->Commentator<-

### The Master of Astrology, Shri Vasudeva
<note>original: "Daivajnavachaspati." This is an honorific title. "Daivajna" refers to an astrologer or one who knows the divine/fate, and "Vachaspati" means a lord of speech or a great scholar.</note>

---

->Publisher<-

<note>Varanasi (also known as Benares or Kashi) is the traditional heart of Sanskrit learning and religious publishing in India. Kachauri Gali is a famous historic lane in the old city known for its bookstores.</note>

->|| Prosperity ||<-

# Moonlight of the Ascendant
## With a Commentary in the Common Language

---

### First Chapter [ 1 ]
### The Auspicious Invocation
<note>original: "Mangalacharanam". A traditional opening prayer to ensure the successful completion of a literary work.</note>

> Having bowed to the Teacher and Ganesha, the author Vasudeva, for the delight of the virtuous, now adorns this *Moonlight of the Ascendant* with a commentary in the common language. <note>original: "bhasha-tikaya". The author refers to the Hindi translation provided alongside the original Sanskrit verses.</note>
> I bow to the Supreme Bliss, the Lord who is the witness of all, who gives life to the beings on this earth when the world is seized by darkness.

I offer my salutations to the Sun God, who is the witness of the entire universe and the embodiment of supreme bliss. For it is he alone who grants life to the living beings of this world when it is enveloped in darkness.

### Now, the Specific Definitions

> Body, Wealth, Brother, Friend, Son, Enemy, Wife,
> Death, Virtue, Action, Income, and Expenditure—these are declared to be the Houses in order. || 1 ||
<term>Bhava: A "house" or "state" in a horoscope. There are twelve houses, each governing specific areas of life.</term>

Body, Wealth, Brother, Friend, Son, Enemy, Wife, Death, Virtue, Action, Income, and Expenditure: these are the names given to the twelve <term>Bhavas</term> (Houses) in their respective order. || 1 ||

> Odd and even, male and female, cruel and gentle by name;
> Movable, fixed, and dual-natured—these are the signs beginning with Aries in sequence. || 2 ||
<term>Rashi: A zodiac sign. There are twelve signs, starting with Aries (Mesha).</term>

Aries, Taurus, Gemini, Cancer, Leo, Virgo, Libra, Scorpio, Sagittarius, Capricorn, Aquarius, and Pisces—these twelve zodiac signs are categorized respectively as odd or even, male or female, cruel or gentle, and movable, fixed, or dual-natured. || 2 ||
<note>This categorization is fundamental for interpreting the character of each sign; for instance, Aries is odd, male, cruel, and movable.</note>

<summary>This opening page of the 'Lagnachandrika' provides a traditional prayer to the Sun and defines the twelve houses of a horoscope and the basic classifications of the zodiac signs.</summary>
<keywords>Lagnachandrika, Invocation, Houses (Bhavas), Zodiac Signs (Rashis), Astrology, Vasudeva, Sun God</keywords>

<page-num>6</page-num>
<header>Moonlight of the Ascendant</header>

## Moonlight of the Ascendant

->The third house is *Duschikya*; the fourth is *Sukha* (Happiness) and *Sadma* (Abode).<-
->It is also known as *Bandhu* (Kin), *Patala* (Subterranean), and *Hibuka*; the fifth is *Dhi* (Intellect). || 3 ||<-

The third house is named *Duschikya*. The fourth house has the names *Sukha*, *Geha* (Home), *Bandhu*, *Patala*, and *Hibuka* <note>original: "hibuka" is a term derived from the Greek "hypogeion," meaning underground</note>. The fifth house is named *Dhi* (Intelligence). || 3 ||

->The seventh is called *Dyuna*, *Asta* (Setting), and *Jamitra*.<-
->The tenth is *Ambara* (Sky) and *Madhya* (Middle); the eighth house is *Chhidra* (Vulnerability). || 4 ||<-

The seventh house is called *Dyuna*, *Asta*, and *Jamitra* <note>original: "jamitra" is derived from the Greek "diametros," referring to the house diametrically opposite the ascendant</note>. The tenth house is called *Ambara* and *Madhya*. The eighth house is named *Chhidra*. || 4 ||

->The eleventh house is *Labha* (Gain) and *Sarvatobhadra*.<-
->The twelfth is *Vyaya* (Loss) and *Rispha*; the ninth and fifth are the *Trikona* (Trines). || 5 ||<-

The eleventh house is called *Labha* and *Sarvatobhadra*. The twelfth house is named *Vyaya* and *Rispha*. The ninth and fifth houses are given the name *Trikona*. || 5 ||

->The third, sixth, tenth, and eleventh are known as *Upachaya*.<-
->The fourth and eighth houses are called *Chaturasra* by the wise. || 6 ||<-

The third, sixth, tenth, and eleventh positions are called *Upachaya* <note>meaning "accumulative" or "increasing" houses, where planets tend to provide better results over time</note>. The fourth and eighth houses are named *Chaturasra*. || 6 ||

->The first, fourth, seventh, and tenth houses are named *Kendra*, *Chatushtaya*, and *Kantaka*.<-
->Following these are the *Panaphara* and *Apoklima*, understood in their respective order. || 7 ||<-

The first, fourth, seventh, and tenth houses are known as *Kendra* (Angular), *Chatushtaya* (Square), and *Kantaka* (Thorn). The second, fifth, eighth, and eleventh houses are called *Panaphara* (Succedent), and the third, sixth, ninth, and twelfth houses are called *Apoklima* (Cadent). || 7 ||

->The *Vargottama* ninth-divisions in movable and other signs are the first, middle, and last.<-
->The *Hora* in odd signs belongs to the Sun and Moon; in even signs, to the Moon and Sun, in order. || 8 ||<-
<term>Vargottama: A "best division" where a planet occupies the same zodiac sign in both the main chart and the ninth sub-division (Navamsha) chart, significantly increasing its strength.</term>
<term>Hora: A division of a zodiac sign into two equal parts of 15 degrees each, used to determine planetary strength and gender of influence.</term>

<vocab>Astrology, Ascendant (Lagna), Houses (Bhava), Happiness and others (Sukha-adi), Increasing Houses (Upachaya), Angular Houses (Kendra), Trines (Trikona), Succedent Houses (Panaphara), Cadent Houses (Apoklima), Moonlight of the Ascendant (Lagnachandrika)</vocab>

<summary>This page lists the various technical Sanskrit and Greek-derived names for the twelve astrological houses and defines their functional groupings, such as the Angular (Kendra) and Succedent (Panaphara) houses. It also introduces the concepts of Vargottama (auspicious sub-divisions) and Hora (half-sign divisions).</summary>
<keywords>Houses, Bhava, Kendra, Trikona, Upachaya, Panaphara, Apoklima, Vargottama, Hora, Astrological nomenclature</keywords>

--- Page 11 ---
<meta>This page continues the technical classification of the zodiac signs and planets, moving from the names of the houses to the specific sub-divisions of the signs, their strength based on time of day, and their manner of rising on the horizon.</meta>

<page-num>7</page-num>
<header>With Language Commentary</header>

The first, fifth, and ninth *Navamsha* <note>original: "navamsha," the 1/9th division of a sign, crucial for determining a planet's true strength</note> are considered *Vargottama* <note>literally "best in the division," referring to a planet that occupies the same zodiac sign in both the main birth chart and the ninth-harmonic chart</note> for Moveable, Fixed, and Dual signs respectively. Specifically, for Moveable signs, the first *Navamsha* is *Vargottama*; for Dual signs, the fifth; and for Fixed signs, the ninth. In odd-numbered signs, the first fifteen degrees belong to the *Hora* of the Sun, followed by the *Hora* of the Moon. In even-numbered signs, the first fifteen degrees belong to the Moon, followed by the Sun.

->The twelve-fold divisions start from their own signs; the Decans belong to the first, fifth, and ninth signs.<-
->The four signs starting with Aries, along with Sagittarius and Capricorn, are known to be strong at night. || 9 ||<-
->Those known as "rising from the back" are not auspicious for starting a journey.<-
->The four starting from Leo, along with Aquarius and Gemini, are strong during the day. || 10 ||<-
->These are "rising from the head," while Pisces is strong at both times and in its sign.<-
->The waning Moon, Sun, Mars, the malefic Rahu, Saturn, and Ketu are the cruel planets. || 11 ||<-

In every sign, the *Dvadasamsha* <note>the 1/12th division of a sign, equal to 2.5 degrees</note> is calculated starting from the sign itself. Every sign also contains three *Drekkanas* <note>original: "drekkana," also known as a Decan, a 10-degree division</note>; the first belongs to the sign itself, the second to the fifth sign from it, and the third to the ninth sign from it. Six signs—Aries, Taurus, Gemini, Cancer, Sagittarius, and Capricorn—are powerful during the night. Among these, excluding Gemini, the others are considered *Prishthodaya* <note>meaning "rising from the back," a classification of signs that appear to rise rear-first on the horizon</note>. These are considered unfavorable for beginning travels. The four signs starting from Leo (Leo, Virgo, Libra, Scorpio), along with Aquarius and Gemini, are strong during the day and are *Shirshodaya* <note>meaning "rising from the head," considered generally auspicious</note>. Among all these, Pisces is considered to have strength at both times (day and night). || 9–11 ||

->Mercury, when joined with these, also becomes a malefic. A *Hora* is said to be half of a sign.<-
->Sun, Moon, Mars, Jupiter; Mercury, Venus, Saturn, and Rahu. || 12 ||<-

The waning Moon, Sun, Mars, Rahu <note>the North Node of the Moon</note>, Saturn, and Ketu <note>the South Node, here called "Shikhi"</note> are considered *Papagraha* <note>literally "sinful" or malefic planets</note>. When Mercury is associated with these, it also acts as a malefic planet. Half of a zodiac sign (15 degrees) is called a *Hora* <note>a term related to the Greek "ora," from which we get the word "hour"</note>. || 12 ||

<vocab>
<term>Navamsha</term>: The ninth-part division of a sign.
<term>Vargottama</term>: A planet in the same sign in both the main and ninth-harmonic charts.
<term>Hora</term>: A 15-degree division (half a sign).
<term>Drekkana</term>: A 10-degree division (one-third of a sign).
<term>Prishthodaya</term>: Signs that rise rear-first.
<term>Shirshodaya</term>: Signs that rise head-first.
<term>Papagraha</term>: Malefic or "cruel" planets.
<term>Chara, Sthira, Dvisvabhava</term>: Moveable, Fixed, and Dual signs.
<term>Ratribali, Dinabali</term>: Signs gaining strength specifically at night or during the day.
</vocab>

<summary>This page defines the sub-divisions of zodiac signs (Navamsha, Hora, Drekkana) and classifies signs and planets by their inherent nature, strength based on time of day, and their directional rising on the horizon.</summary>
<keywords>Vargottama, Hora, Decans, Malefic planets, Prishthodaya, Shirshodaya, Night-strong signs, Day-strong signs, Mercury, Rahu, Ketu</keywords>

--- Page 12 ---
<meta>This page continues from the previous discussion on planetary strengths and sign divisions (Navamsha and Drekkana). It transitions from the physical characteristics of the signs to the relationships between planets (friendship and enmity) and their states of dignity (exaltation and debilitation), concluding with the life outcomes produced by these planetary positions.</meta>

<page-num>4</page-num>
<header>Moonlight of the Ascendant</header>

->Among themselves, four are remembered as friends; among others, they are known as enemies. || 13 ||<-

The Sun, Moon, Mars, Jupiter, Mercury, Venus, Saturn, and Rahu <term>Rahu: the North Node of the Moon, considered a "shadow planet" in Vedic astrology</term> have mutual friendships. For example: the Sun, Moon, and Jupiter are friends, but if Mercury, Venus, Saturn, and Rahu are placed with them, they become enemies. || 13 ||

->The Sun in Aries, the Moon in Taurus, and the son of the Earth in Capricorn;<-
->The son of Rohini in Virgo, Jupiter in Cancer, and Venus in the Fish; || 14 ||<-
->Saturn is exalted in Libra, and the son of Simhika in Gemini.<-
->The seventh sign from the exaltation is the sign of debilitation, whether in the main sign or the ninth-part division. || 15 ||<-

The Sun in Aries, the Moon in Taurus, and Mars <note>original: "mahisutah," literally 'son of the Earth'</note> in Capricorn are exalted <note>original: "uccha," the sign where a planet is at its maximum strength</note>. Mercury <note>original: "rohiniputrah," 'son of Rohini'</note> in the sign of Virgo, Jupiter in Cancer, Venus in Pisces <note>original: "jhasha," meaning 'the fish'</note>, Saturn in Libra, and Rahu <note>original: "simhikasutah," 'son of Simhika'</note> in Gemini are exalted. The seventh sign from each exaltation sign is its sign of debilitation <note>original: "nicha," the sign where a planet is at its weakest</note>. For example—if the Sun is exalted in Aries, then in the seventh sign from it, Libra, the Sun will be debilitated. This sequence of exaltation and debilitation also applies to the ninth-division chart <note>original: "navamsha," a subtle divisional chart used to confirm a planet's true power</note>. || 14-15 ||

### Now, the Auspicious and Inauspicious Combinations

->One becomes a seeker, an enjoyer, wealthy, a leader, or a ruler of a region;<-
->A king or a universal emperor is born when the Sun and other planets are in their signs of exaltation. || 1 ||<-

Depending on which of the Sun and other planets are exalted, a person becomes a seeker, an enjoyer, wealthy, and so on. For example—if the Sun is exalted, one is wealthy; if the Moon is in its exaltation sign, one is an enjoyer of pleasures; if Mars is in its exaltation sign, one is wealthy; if Mercury is in its exaltation sign, one is a leader; if Jupiter is in its exaltation sign, one is a regional ruler; if Venus is in its exaltation sign, one becomes a king; and if Saturn is in its exaltation sign, one becomes a universal emperor. || 1 ||

->With three planets in their own signs, one becomes a minister; with three exalted, a ruler of men;<-
->With three debilitated, one becomes a servant; with three combust, one becomes dull-witted. || 2 ||<-

<summary>This page defines the friendly and inimical relationships between planets and lists the specific zodiac signs where each planet reaches its peak strength (exaltation) or greatest weakness (debilitation), along with the worldly status these positions confer on a person.</summary>
<keywords>planetary friendship, exaltation, debilitation, Lagna Chandrika, Sun in Aries, Moon in Taurus, Rahu, planetary dignity, Yogas</keywords>

--- Page 13 ---
<meta>This page continues from the discussion of planetary dignities—exaltation and debilitation—and transitions into the practical effects of these planetary strengths on an individual's destiny, specifically identifying "Royal Combinations" (Raja Yogas) that lead to status and power.</meta>

<page-num>1</page-num>
<header>With Vernacular Commentary</header>

If in someone’s birth chart <term>Birth Chart: a map of the sky at the moment of birth, known as a 'Janma Kundali'</term> three planets are "healthy"—meaning they are situated with full strength—that person becomes a minister to a king. If three planets are in their sign of debilitation <note>original: "nicha," the sign where a planet is at its weakest</note>, they become a servant; and if three planets are in incompatible or "discordant" positions, the child will possess a dull intellect. || 2 ||

> ->Risen, residing in its own home, or even situated in the home of a friend;<-
> ->In a friend’s divisional charts or aspected by a friend; that planet is remembered as strong. || 3 ||<-

A planet that is "risen" <note>original: "udita," meaning it is visible and not "combust" or obscured by the Sun's proximity</note>, is located in its own zodiac sign or the sign of a friendly planet, is in the "six-fold divisions" <term>Shadvarga: a system of dividing a sign into six parts to calculate subtle planetary strength</term> of a friendly planet, or is aspected <note>original: "drishta," meaning it receives the 'gaze' or influence of another planet</note> by a friend, is considered powerful. || 3 ||

> ->Aspected by its strong Lord, and by strong benefic planets;<-
> ->Neither aspected by nor joined with malefic planets—that house is remembered as strong. || 4 ||<-

When a "house" <term>Bhava: one of the twelve sectors of the birth chart representing specific life areas</term> is aspected by its own strong planetary ruler or by strong "benefic" <note>original: "shubha," naturally favorable planets like Jupiter or Venus</note> planets, and is not influenced by the gaze or presence of "malefic" <note>original: "papa," naturally harsh planets like Mars or Saturn</note> planets, that house is called strong. || 4 ||

> ->Mercury and the Sun in the tenth, Mars and Rahu in the sixth;<-
> ->A person born in this royal combination becomes a leader among men. || 5 ||<-

If, counting from the Ascendant, Mercury and the Sun are in the tenth house <note>the house of career and public life</note>, and Mars and Rahu are in the sixth house, this constitutes a "Royal Combination" <term>Raja Yoga: a planetary alignment indicating high status, power, or success</term>. A person born under this alignment becomes a leader among men or a commander of an army. || 5 ||

> ->Beginning with Jupiter and ending with Saturn, with planets in between without interruption;<-
> ->Know this to be a Royal Combination, joined with the strength of family and wealth. || 6 ||<-

Starting from the house where Jupiter is located, if all the other planets are placed continuously in the houses following it up to where Saturn is located, and the ruler of the second house <note>original: "kutumbapala," the protector of family/wealth</note> is strong, this generally results in a Royal Combination. || 6 ||

> ->When Jupiter is in the third house and Venus is in the eighth house;<-
> ->With planets continuously in between, one certainly becomes a king. || 7 ||<-

<summary>This page outlines the conditions for planetary strength and introduces several "Raja Yogas" (Royal Combinations) that predict high social status, leadership, or royal authority based on planetary placement.</summary>
<keywords>birth chart, planetary strength, Raja Yoga, Mercury, Sun, Mars, Rahu, Jupiter, Saturn, leadership, benefic planets, malefic planets</keywords>

--- Page 14 ---
<meta>This page continues the technical classification of planetary arrangements, moving from general strength to specific "Raja Yogas" (Royal Combinations) that predict high status, sovereignty, and the specific life challenges—such as critical years of health—that accompany these powerful horoscopes.</meta>

<page-num>10</page-num>
<header>The Moonlight of the Ascendant</header>

If Jupiter is in the third house and Venus is in the eighth house, with the remaining planets situated between them, that person will certainly become a king. || 7 ||

> ->If Jupiter is in Taurus, the Moon in Gemini, Mars in Capricorn;<-
> ->Saturn in Leo, and both Mercury and the Sun are in Virgo;<-
> ->Venus in Libra—this forms a Royal Combination.<-
> ->A person born under this alignment becomes a Great King. || 8-9 ||<-

If Jupiter <note>original: "Jiva"</note> is in Taurus, the Moon <note>original: "Sudharashmi"</note> is in Gemini, Mars is in Capricorn, Saturn is in Leo, Mercury and the Sun are in Virgo, and Venus <note>original: "Asuracharya"</note> is in the sign of Libra, then a child born in such a planetary arrangement becomes a universal sovereign <term>Sarvabhauma: a supreme ruler or emperor of the known world</term>. || 8-9 ||

> ->If the person survives their eighth and twelfth years;<-
> ->Then they become a universal king and a protector of the world. || 10 ||<-

A child born in the aforementioned Royal Combination who survives past their eighth and twelfth years will become a universal sovereign who protects the entire world. The deeper meaning is that the eighth and twelfth years are inauspicious <note>original: "anishtakaraka"</note> or life-threatening for such an individual. || 10 ||

> ->When Jupiter alone is in the Ascendant, then all combinations become auspicious;<-
> ->The person becomes long-lived, highly honored, a leader, and a warrior. || 11 ||<-

If even an exalted <term>Exalted: "Uccha," the sign where a planet reaches its maximum potential</term> Jupiter alone is situated in the Ascendant <term>Lagna: the zodiac sign rising on the eastern horizon at the moment of birth</term>, then all other planetary combinations become favorable. A child born in such a state will have a long life, be respected by royalty, and become a commander of armies. || 11 ||

> ->Mars and Venus in Sagittarius, Jupiter in Pisces, Mercury in Libra;<-
> ->Saturn and the Moon in their signs of debilitation—one becomes a king devoid of wealth. || 12 ||<-

If Mars and Venus are in the sign of Sagittarius, Jupiter is in Pisces, Mercury is in Libra, and Saturn and the Moon are located in their signs of debilitation <note>original: "nicha rashi," the signs where these planets are at their weakest: Aries for Saturn and Scorpio for the Moon</note>, the person born under this combination will be a "king" in status but will lack wealth. Alternatively, even if born into a wealthy lineage, they will become destitute. || 12 ||

<page-num>11</page-num>
<header>With Language Commentary</header>

> The giver and the enjoyer, renowned and respected as a leader of the realm;
> Venus in Pisces, Mercury in the end, Rahu in wealth, and the Sun in the Ascendant. || 13 ||

If Venus <note>original: "Shukra"</note> is in the sign of Pisces, Mercury <note>original: "Budha"</note> is in the twelfth house <note>original: "vyaya bhava," the house of loss or the "end"</note>, Rahu is in the second house <term>Dhana-bhava: the house of wealth and assets</term>, and the Sun is in the Ascendant <term>Lagna: the zodiac sign rising on the eastern horizon at birth</term>, the child born under this combination will be generous, a connoisseur of pleasures, a famous and respected leader of the state, and a lord of the land. || 13 ||

> If Mars is in the third, a king is born in this combination;
> When Jupiter is in the third and the Moon is in the house of gains,
> He is a king among kings, famous, and the light of his lineage. || 14 ||

If Jupiter <note>original: "Jiva"</note> is in the third house from the Ascendant and the Moon is in the eleventh house <term>Labha-sthana: the house of gains and ambitions</term>, a child born in such an alignment becomes a king among kings, highly honored, and a lord of the earth. He is known as the "light of his family" <note>original: "kuladipaka," one who brings glory to their ancestors</note>. || 14 ||

> If benefic planets are in auspicious signs and situated in the angles,
> Then the child will certainly perform righteous deeds. || 15 ||

If all the benefic planets <note>planets like Jupiter, Venus, and Mercury</note> are placed in signs owned by other benefics and occupy the angular houses <term>Kendra: the 1st, 4th, 7th, and 10th houses, considered the pillars of a horoscope</term>, then the child born in this combination remains devoted to performing good and auspicious works. || 15 ||

> If the gentle planets are in their exalted positions and situated in the angles,
> Sovereignty is certain for him, and he shall be the sustainer of his kinsmen. || 16 ||

Whosoever has all the gentle planets <note>original: "saumya graha"</note> in their exalted positions <term>Uccha: the specific sign where a planet exerts its maximum positive influence</term> while falling within the angular houses will surely become a ruler and the provider for his entire family line. || 16 ||

> When planets are in the house of wealth, the house of loss, the Ascendant, and the seventh,
> This is known as the Parasol Combination; he becomes a leader of men. || 17 ||

When all the planets are situated in the second, twelfth, first, and seventh houses, it forms the "Parasol Combination" <term>Chhatra-yoga: a planetary pattern named after the royal umbrella, symbolizing protection and high status</term>. A child born of this combination becomes a commander or a leader of armies. || 17 ||

<page-num>12</page-num>
<header>The Moon-Ray of the Ascendant</header>

> When four planets are placed anywhere alongside the Sun or the Moon,
> This is called the *Mala* Yoga; it grants sovereignty and wealth. || 18 ||

If four planets are situated in any house together with either the Sun or the Moon, the "Garland Alignment" <term>Mala-yoga: a combination suggesting a 'garland' of planetary influences</term> is formed. This alignment brings about the acquisition of state power and great riches. || 18 ||

> When Jupiter, Mercury, and Saturn are each placed in their own signs,
> The one born under this certainly possesses long life and prosperity. || 19 ||

If, in the birth chart <note>original: "Janma Kundali"</note>, Jupiter <note>original: "Jiva"</note> is seated in Sagittarius or Pisces, Mercury <note>original: "Budha"</note> is in Gemini or Virgo, and Saturn <note>original: "Sauri"</note> is in Capricorn or Aquarius, the child born in this alignment will be long-lived and wealthy. <note>In Vedic astrology, a planet is strongest when "at home" in the zodiac signs it rules.</note> || 19 ||

> When Jupiter, Venus, and the Moon are all in Pisces,
> The one born there shall have a kingdom and a wife with many sons. || 20 ||

If Jupiter, Venus <note>original: "Shukra"</note>, and the Moon are all positioned in the sign of Pisces, the child born in this alignment becomes a ruler, and his wife will be blessed with many sons. || 20 ||

> When Jupiter is in the fifth house and the Moon is in the tenth,
> He is honored, highly intelligent, ascetic, and a master of his senses. || 21 ||

If Jupiter is in the fifth house from the Ascendant <note>the house of intellect and children</note> and the Moon is in the tenth house <note>the house of career and public standing</note>, the child born under such an alignment will be a king, exceptionally wise, and a "conqueror of senses" <term>Jitendriya: one who has complete control over their desires and physical impulses</term>. || 21 ||

> If Jupiter is in Leo, and the other planets are in Libra, Scorpio,
> Sagittarius, and Capricorn, then the person born becomes a ruler of the land. || 22 ||

If Jupiter is placed in the sign of Leo, and all other planets are distributed across the signs of Libra, Scorpio, Sagittarius, and Capricorn, the child will become the "enjoyer of the country" and a lord of the earth. || 22 ||

<header>With the Language Commentary</header>

> When Saturn is positioned in the Ascendant within the signs of Libra, Sagittarius, or Pisces, 
> It causes the birth of a king; however, in any other sign, the person is poor. || 23 ||

If Saturn <note>original: "Shanaishvara"</note> is present in the first house <term>Lagna: the Ascendant or the house representing the self and the body</term> while situated in the signs of Libra, Sagittarius, or Pisces, the child will be a king. If Saturn is placed in the Ascendant in any other zodiac sign, the child will be destitute. || 23 ||

> When Mercury is in the house of knowledge and the Moon is in the house of action, 
> And when a benefic is in the house of piety, a king is born in this alignment. || 24 ||

If Mercury <note>original: "Budha"</note> is in the fifth house <note>the house of intellect and children</note>, the Moon is in the tenth house <note>the house of profession and public life</note>, and a benefic planet <note>original: "Saumya," referring to auspicious planets like Jupiter or Venus</note> is located in the ninth house <note>the house of fortune and religion</note>, the child born under such an alignment becomes as illustrious as a king. If these planets are also in their exalted signs or their own "home" signs, this alignment provides its full, most powerful results. || 24 ||

> When all the planets are in Capricorn, Sagittarius, Pisces, 
> Taurus, Gemini, and Aries, the person born becomes a famous king. || 25 ||

If all the planets are distributed across the signs of Capricorn <note>original: "Makara"</note>, Sagittarius <note>original: "Karmuka"</note>, Pisces, Taurus, Gemini, and Aries <note>original: "Kriya"</note>, the child will specifically become a world-renowned ruler. || 25 ||

> If Rahu is joined with Mercury, Venus, Jupiter, and Saturn 
> In the four pillars, it grants wealth, health, sons, and a highly honored home. || 26 ||

If Rahu <note>the North Node of the Moon</note> is positioned alongside Mercury, Venus <note>original: "Bhargava"</note>, Jupiter <note>original: "Jiva"</note>, and Saturn <note>original: "Arki"</note> in the "pillars" <term>Kendra: the four central houses of a chart (1st, 4th, 7th, and 10th) which represent the foundations of life</term>, the result is excellent. A child born in this alignment will be wealthy, healthy, blessed with sons, and highly respected. || 26 ||

> When Venus, Jupiter, the Moon, and the Son of Earth 
> Are in the fourth house with the Sun and Saturn, one certainly becomes a king. || 27 ||

If Venus, Jupiter, the Moon, and Mars <note>original: "Dharasuta," literally "Son of the Earth"</note> are seated in the fourth house <note>representing the home and happiness</note> joined with the Sun and Saturn, the child will certainly become a ruler. || 27 ||

<page-num>१४</page-num>
<header>Moonlight on the Ascendant</header>

->If malefic planets are in the eighth and twelfth houses, and both malefic and benefic planets are in the middle;<-
->The person born in this Alignment of Kings lives for forty years. || 28 ||<-

If <term>malefic planets: "Krura Grahas" like Mars or Saturn, associated with harshness</term> are situated in the eighth and <term>twelfth houses: the "Vyaya Bhava" or house of loss</term>, and both <term>benefic: "Saumya," gentle or auspicious planets</term> and malefic planets are situated in the ninth, tenth, and eleventh houses <note>these houses represent fortune, profession, and gains respectively</note>, then that person becomes a king. However, if mixed malefic and benefic planets are situated in those middle positions, their lifespan will be 40 years. || 28 ||

->If Saturn and the Moon are in the Ascendant, and Jupiter and the Sun are in the trines;<-
->If Mars is in the house of action, one born in this alignment becomes a king. || 29 ||<-

If Saturn <note>original: "Sauri"</note> and the Moon are in the <term>Ascendant: "Lagna," the first house representing the self</term>, Jupiter <note>original: "Jiva"</note> and the Sun <note>original: "Bhaskara"</note> are in the <term>trines: "Trikona," the auspicious 1st, 5th, or 9th houses</term>, and Mars <note>original: "Bhauma"</note> is situated in the <term>tenth house: "Karma-sthana," the house of career and public deeds</term>, the child born in this alignment becomes a king. || 29 ||

->When the Sun is in the ninth house and situated in its own home;<-
->His brother does not live, but even alone, he is equal to a king. || 30 ||<-

If the Sun is in the <term>ninth house: the house of fate and father</term> while situated in its own zodiac sign <note>Leo</note>, then the siblings of the subject will perish. Even while remaining alone <note>without the support of brothers</note>, the person becomes equal to a ruler. || 30 ||

->When planets are in the second, third, fourth, fifth, sixth, and tenth houses;<-
->One should recognize this as an Alignment of Kings; the person born there becomes a king. || 31 ||<-

If planets are situated in the second, third, fourth, fifth, sixth, and tenth houses from the birth Ascendant, it should be called a <term>Rajayoga: an astrological combination for power and status</term>. A child born in this alignment becomes a king. || 31 ||

->If a malefic is in the Ascendant, a malefic in the twelfth, and a malefic in the house of wealth;<-
->And if a malefic is in the seventh house, it brings destruction to the family. || 32 ||<-

If there is a malefic planet in the Ascendant, a malefic in the <term>twelfth house: the house of expenditure</term>, a malefic in the <term>second house: "Dhana Bhava," the house of wealth</term>, and also a malefic planet in the <term>seventh house: the house of marriage and partners</term>, then the child born in this alignment causes the destruction of his entire family. || 32 ||

<header>With Commentary in Common Language</header>

->If a malefic planet is in the Ascendant, a benefic in the twelfth, and a malefic in the second;<-
->One born in this Royal Alignment becomes a pauper, destitute of wealth. || 33 ||<-

If a <term>malefic planet: "Krura Graha," such as Mars or Saturn</term> is in the <term>Ascendant: "Lagna," the first house of the birth chart</term>, a <term>benefic planet: "Saumya Graha," such as Jupiter or Venus</term> is in the <term>twelfth house: "Vyaya Bhava," the house of expenditure and loss</term>, and another malefic planet is in the second house <note>the house of accumulated wealth</note>, then the child born under this <term>Rajayoga: "Royal Alignment," typically implying power</term> will be poor and without money, even if they are born into a royal family. || 33 ||

->When Saturn and the Moon are in Sagittarius and Jupiter is in Aries;<-
->If Rahu and Venus are in the tenth, one born in this Royal Alignment becomes a king. || 34 ||<-

If Saturn <note>original: "Sauri"</note> and the Moon are in the sign of <term>Sagittarius: "Chapa," the bow</term>, Jupiter <note>original: "Jiva"</note> is in <term>Aries: "Mesha"</term>, and Rahu and Venus are situated in the <term>tenth house: the house of career and public status</term>, the child born in this alignment becomes a king. || 34 ||

->If Jupiter is in Leo, Venus in Virgo, and Saturn in Gemini;<-
->With Mars in his own sign in the fourth house, that man becomes a leader. || 35 ||<-

If Jupiter is in the sign of <term>Leo: "Simha"</term>, Venus <note>original: "Bhargava"</note> is in <term>Virgo: "Kanya"</term>, Saturn is in <term>Gemini: "Mithuna"</term>, and Mars <note>original: "Bhauma"</note> is in the <term>fourth house: "Hibuka"</term> while positioned in its own zodiac sign <note>Aries or Scorpio</note>, then the child born in this alignment becomes a general, a commander, or a chief leader. || 35 ||

->Saturn and Moon in Virgo, Jupiter in Leo, Rahu in Aquarius;<-
->With Mars in Capricorn, one born there becomes a protector of the world. || 36 ||<-

If Saturn and the Moon are in the sign of Virgo, Jupiter is in Leo, Rahu <note>original: "Tamas," meaning darkness</note> is in <term>Aquarius: "Ghata," the water-pot</term>, and Mars is in <term>Capricorn: "Makara"</term>, the child born at that time becomes a <term>Chakravarti: a "Universal Ruler" or sovereign of the world</term>. || 36 ||

->Venus, Jupiter, Sun, and Mars in Sagittarius, Capricorn, and Aquarius;<-
->And in Pisces—in the twentieth year, the one born becomes capable of all deeds. || 37 ||<-

If the planets Venus, Jupiter, Sun, and Mars are placed respectively in the signs of Sagittarius, Capricorn, Aquarius, and Pisces <note>meaning Venus in Sagittarius, Jupiter in Capricorn, etc.</note>, then by the age of twenty, the child becomes capable of performing all types of work and fulfilling his duties. || 37 ||

<meta>This page continues the discussion of planetary alignments (Yogas) from the Lagnachandrika. While the previous page provided was numbered 95 and appeared to be from a later section or a different edition, this page is numbered 16 and continues the sequence of verses (38-42) regarding auspicious and challenging "Royal Alignments."</meta>

<page-num>16</page-num>
<header>Moonbeams of the Ascendant</header>

->When benefic and malefic planets are situated in the four angular houses;<-
->This is the "Four Oceans Alignment," which bestows power and wealth. || 38 ||<-

If <term>benefic: "Saumya," gentle planets like Jupiter, Venus, or Mercury</term> and <term>malefic: "Papa," harsh planets like Mars or Saturn</term> are located in the <term>angular houses: "Kendra," the 1st, 4th, 7th, and 10th houses of a birth chart</term>, the "Four Oceans Alignment" (<term>Chatuh-Sagara Yoga</term>) is formed. This alignment grants the person political power and great riches. || 38 ||

---

->With Jupiter in the Cancer Ascendant, and the Moon, Mercury, and Venus in the eleventh house;<-
->And the Sun in Aries, one born becomes a king who protects the whole world. || 39 ||<-

If the <term>Ascendant: "Lagna," the sign rising on the eastern horizon at birth</term> is <term>Cancer: "Karka"</term> and contains Jupiter <note>original: "Jiva"</note>, while the Moon, Mercury <note>original: "Jna"</note>, and Venus <note>original: "Bhargava"</note> are located in the <term>eleventh house: "Labha Bhava," the house of gains</term>, and the Sun <note>original: "Bhanu"</note> is in <term>Aries: "Mesha"</term>, then the child born will be a protector of the world or a great emperor. || 39 ||

---

->When Jupiter, Mercury, Venus, and the Moon are in the house of action;<-
->All tasks are successfully completed, and the man is honored by kings. || 40 ||<-

If Jupiter, Mercury, Venus, and the Moon—all four of these planets—are seated in the <term>tenth house: "Karma Sthana," the house of career and public deeds</term>, the person born will see all their endeavors succeed and will be highly respected among rulers. || 40 ||

---

->In the sixth, eighth, fifth, ninth, and twelfth houses;<-
->An alignment of benefic and malefic planets makes one honored by kings, yet troubled. || 41 ||<-

If both <term>benefic: "Saumya"</term> and <term>malefic: "Krura," meaning cruel or harsh</term> planets are located respectively in the sixth, eighth, ninth, and twelfth houses <note>The Sanskrit verse also includes the fifth house: "Panchame"</note> from the birth Ascendant, the individual will be honored by kings, yet their life will be spent in hardship <note>original: "Sakantaka," literally "with thorns"</note>. || 41 ||

---

->When in the fifth, sixth, eighth, and ninth houses respectively;<-
->Mars, Rahu, Venus, and the Sun are placed, one born is the lamp of their lineage. || 42 ||<-

If Mars <note>original: "Bhauma"</note>, Rahu <note>the North Node of the Moon</note>, Venus <note>original: "Sita"</note>, and the Sun <note>original: "Arka"</note> are situated in the fifth, sixth, eighth, and ninth houses in that specific order, the child born becomes the "Lamp of the Family" (<term>Kula-dipaka</term>), bringing prestige and honor to their entire lineage. || 42 ||

<summary>This page details five specific planetary configurations (Yogas) that predict royal status, worldly success, or family prestige, including the notable "Four Oceans Alignment" and the "Lamp of the Family" Yoga.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the catalog of specific planetary alignments (Yogas) that indicate a person will achieve royal status or significant wealth. It follows the numbering sequence from the previous page, moving from verse 42 to verses 43 through 48.</meta>

<page-num>17</page-num>
<header>With Vernacular Commentary</header>

->When Saturn and the Moon are in the Ascendant, and Venus is in the eighth house;<-
->The one born then becomes a proud king, ever devoted to his wife. || 43 ||<-

If <term>Saturn: "Sauri"</term> and the <term>Moon: "Chandra"</term> are located in the <term>Ascendant: "Lagna," the first house of the birth chart</term>, and <term>Venus: "Bhargava"</term> is in the <term>eighth house: "Ashtama Bhava," often associated with longevity and transformation</term>, the child born will become a king. He will be a man of great self-respect and will remain constantly devoted to his wife. || 43 ||

---

->When Rahu is situated in Gemini and the Son of the Earth is in Leo;<-
->One born here, as a king, obtains the entirety of his father’s wealth. || 44 ||<-

If the shadow planet <term>Rahu</term> is in <term>Gemini: "Mithuna"</term> and <term>Mars: "Bhuminandana," literally 'Son of the Earth'</term> is in <term>Leo: "Simha"</term>, the person born under this alignment inherits his father's entire estate and achieves the status of a ruler. || 44 ||

---

->If the Sun is joined with the Moon in the first half of Sagittarius;<-
->And a strong Saturn is in the Ascendant, and Mars is in Capricorn; || 45 ||<-
->A man born in this alignment becomes a Great King;<-
->Other kings bow to his feet from afar because of his majesty. || 46 ||<-

When, at the time of birth, the Moon <note>original: "Shashi"</note> is joined with the <term>Sun: "Surya"</term> in the first fifteen degrees <note>original: "ardha-bhaga," meaning the first half or 15 degrees of a 30-degree sign</note> of <term>Sagittarius: "Chapa"</term>, while a powerful Saturn <note>original: "Manda"</note> is seated in the Ascendant and Mars <note>original: "Kuja"</note> is in <term>Capricorn: "Makara"</term>, the child born becomes a <term>Chakravarti: an emperor or universal ruler</term>. Due to his immense power and glory, other kings offer their respects at his feet from a distance. || 45-46 ||

---

->When the Sun, seeking its exaltation, is situated in a trine;<-
->Even if born in a lowly family, he becomes a king filled with wealth. || 47 ||<-

If the <term>Sun: "Savita"</term> is "seeking its exaltation" <note>original: "uchcha-abhilashi," referring to the Sun being in the signs leading up to its highest point in Aries</note> and is positioned in a <term>trine: "Trikona," the 5th or 9th houses</term>, then even a child born into a humble or disadvantaged family will become a wealthy and powerful ruler. || 47 ||

---

->When all the planets are in the eleventh or the tenth house;<-
->In the Ascendant or opposite it, they are proclaimed as "Producers." || 48 ||<-

If all the planets are situated in the <term>eleventh house: "Ekadasha," the house of gains</term> or the <term>tenth house: "Dashama," the house of career</term>, they are considered highly significant. Whether in the Ascendant or in the house directly opposite it <note>the 7th house</note>, they are traditionally called <term>Karakas: "Producers" or significant indicators of success</term>. || 48 ||

<summary>This page outlines specific planetary configurations (Yogas) that predict the birth of a king or a wealthy individual, including alignments involving Saturn, Rahu, and the Sun in specific zodiac signs. It emphasizes that certain celestial positions can overcome a humble birth to grant high status and inheritance.</summary>
<keywords>Jyotish, Vedic Astrology, Yoga, Royalty, Wealth, Saturn, Rahu, Mars, Sun, Sagittarius, Inheritance, Karaka</keywords>

--- Page 22 ---
<meta>This page continues the catalog of specific planetary alignments (Yogas) that indicate a person will achieve royal status or significant wealth. It follows the numbering sequence from the previous page, moving from the description of planetary placements in specific signs to named auspicious combinations.</meta>

<page-num>18</page-num>
<header>Lagnachandrika (Moonlight of the Ascendant)</header>

->One born in the <term>Karaka Yoga: "Significator Alignment"</term>, even if of low birth, attains kingship.<-
->If born within a royal dynasty, he undoubtedly becomes a king. || 48 ||<-

When all planets are situated in the eleventh, the tenth, or the <term>Ascendant: "Lagna"</term> itself, or in the <term>seventh house: "Saptama Bhava"</term> directly opposite the Ascendant, they are called <term>Karaka: "Significators"</term>. A child born under this Karaka Yoga becomes wealthy even if they were born in poverty; and a child born into a royal family will certainly become a ruler. || 48-49 ||

->If planets fall in an uninterrupted sequence starting from the Ascendant or another house,<-
->It is known as <term>Ekavali: "Single String"</term>; such a man becomes a Great King. || 50 ||<-

If all the planets fall in consecutive houses starting from the Ascendant or any other house—meaning no house in the sequence is left empty—then the <term>Ekavali Yoga</term> is formed. A child born in this Ekavali alignment becomes a king. || 50 ||

->When Venus is in the place of wealth, Jupiter is in the tenth,<-
->And the <term>Son of Simhika: "Simhikaputra," a poetic name for Rahu</term> is in the sixth, he becomes a valorous king. || 51 ||<-

If <term>Venus: "Shukra"</term> is in the second house <note>associated with accumulated wealth</note>, <term>Jupiter: "Brihaspati"</term> is in the tenth house, and <term>Rahu</term> is in the sixth house, the child born will become a king through his own prowess and valor. || 51 ||

->If four planets, whether malefic or benefic, are gathered in a single place—<-
->Specifically in the houses of the brother, the intellect, the trine, or the Ascendant lord—this becomes a <term>Rajayoga: "Royal Union"</term>. || 52 ||<-

If four planets (whether naturally cruel or gentle) are seated together in any one of these houses: the 3rd, 5th, 9th, 1st, or 2nd, the person born in this alignment generally becomes a king. || 52 ||

->When planets are situated in the Trines, the Seventh, or the Ascendant,<-
->Know it as <term>Hamsa Yoga: "Swan Alignment"</term>; he becomes the protector of his own lineage. || 53 ||<-

If all planets are positioned in the <term>Trines: "Trikona," the 5th and 9th houses</term>, or in the seventh house, or in the Ascendant, it is called <term>Hamsa Yoga</term>. One born under this becomes the provider and protector of his kinsmen. || 53 ||

<summary>This page details several prestigious planetary combinations (Yogas) such as Karaka, Ekavali, and Hamsa Yoga, which predict that an individual will rise to royal status, achieve great wealth, or become a pillar of their family.</summary>
<keywords>Karaka Yoga, Ekavali Yoga, Rajayoga, Hamsa Yoga, Rahu, planetary alignments, Vedic astrology, kingship</keywords>

<header>With Commentary in the Common Language</header>

**When the Moon, excluding Scorpio, is aspected by all planets,**
**And is in the sixth, eighth, or seventh house, he is a long-lived ruler of the earth. || 54 ||**
If the <term>Moon: "Chandra"</term> is situated in any zodiac sign except <term>Scorpio: "Vrishchika"</term> within the sixth, eighth, or seventh house and is <note>aspected: "seen" by the planetary influence</note> by all other planets, the person born will have a <term>long life: "Dirghayu"</term> and be a <term>ruler of the earth: "Prithvipati"</term>. || 54 ||

**When planets are in the sixth, eighth, twelfth, and second houses,**
**In this "Throne Alignment," he shall sit upon a royal throne. || 55 ||**
If all planets are positioned specifically in the sixth, eighth, twelfth, and second houses from the <term>Ascendant: "Lagna"</term>, it forms the <term>Simhasana Yoga: "Throne Alignment"</term>. A person born under this alignment sits upon a royal throne or becomes extremely wealthy. || 55 ||

**Venus and Mercury are not in the Ascendant, Jupiter is not in the Angles,**
**Mars is not in the tenth—to what end was he born? || 56 ||**
If <term>Venus: "Shukra"</term> and <term>Mercury: "Budha"</term> are absent from the Ascendant, <term>Jupiter: "Brihaspati"</term> is not in an <term>Angle: "Kendra" (the 1st, 4th, 7th, or 10th houses)</term>, and <term>Mars: "Mangala/Angaraka"</term> is not in the tenth house, then what is the use of that man's birth? <note>original: "kim karanat"</note> In other words, he becomes destitute. || 56 ||

**If cruel planets dwell in the eighth and gentle planets in the Ascendant,**
**In this "Banner Alignment," the man born becomes a leader. || 57 ||**
If <term>malefic: "paapa-graha" or "cruel"</term> planets are in the eighth house and <term>benefic: "shubha-graha" or "gentle"</term> planets are located in the Ascendant, the alignment called <term>Dhvaja Yoga: "Banner Alignment"</term> is formed. A person born in this alignment becomes a sovereign or leader. || 57 ||

**When malefic planets are in the sixth house and benefics in the Angles,**
**He achieves all success and becomes honored by kings. || 58 ||**
If malefic planets are in the sixth house from the Ascendant and benefic planets are in the <term>Angles: "Kendras"</term>, then all his tasks will be successfully completed and he will be <term>honored by royalty: "Rajamanya"</term>. || 58 ||

<vocab>Long-lived, Ruler of the Earth, Throne Alignment, Banner Alignment, Universal Success, Honored by Kings, Scorpio, Cardinal Houses (Angles), Jupiter, Venus, Mercury, Mars, Malefic Planets, Benefic Planets</vocab>

<header>Moonlight of the Ascendant</header>

<note>The right edge of the original page is slightly cropped, affecting some words in the Hindi commentary, which have been reconstructed here based on the Sanskrit verses.</note>

# Moonlight of the Ascendant

**When the Sun is in the Aries Ascendant, Jupiter is in the fourth house, and Mars is in the tenth, the person born shall become a ruler of the entire world. || 59 ||**
If birth occurs in the <term>Aries Ascendant: "Mesha Lagna"</term> and the <term>Sun: "Ravi"</term> is positioned there, while <term>Jupiter: "Brihaspati"</term> is in the fourth house and <term>Mars: "Mangala"</term> is in the tenth, that child shall be a king who rules the world. || 59 ||

**If Saturn and the Moon are in the Ascendant, Jupiter and the Sun are in the trines, and Mars is in the house of action, this is known as a Royal Alignment. || 60 ||**
If <term>Saturn: "Shani/Sauri"</term> and the <term>Moon: "Chandra"</term> are in the birth Ascendant, and <term>Jupiter: "Jiva"</term> and the <term>Sun: "Bhaskara"</term> are in the <term>trines: "Trikona"</term> (the 5th or 9th houses), and Mars is in the tenth house <note>the house of career or action</note>, it is called <term>Rajayoga: "Royal Alignment"</term>. || 60 ||

**A gentle planet situated in its sign of exaltation in an angle makes one a master of royal fortune. A cruel planet in its sign of exaltation in an angle makes one a king, but one devoid of wealth. || 61 ||**
If <term>benefic planets: "Saumya graha"</term> are in their <term>sign of exaltation: "Uccha-rashi"</term> within the <term>angles: "Kendra"</term> (the 1st, 4th, 7th, or 10th houses), the person becomes the master of a kingdom or great wealth. If <term>malefic planets: "Papa graha"</term> are seated in their sign of exaltation within the angles, the person may be a king but will be <note>impoverished: "dhan-varjit"</note>, lacking significant financial resources. || 61 ||

**If a powerful gentle planet situated in an angle aspects the Ascendant, it destroys all misfortunes, just as the rising sun destroys darkness. || 62 ||**
If a powerful benefic planet is situated in an angle and <note>aspects: "sees"</note> the Ascendant, it destroys all <term>afflictions: "Anishta"</term>. This is just as the darkness is dispelled by the rising sun. || 62 ||

**If Rahu and Saturn are in the house of death, the woman is barren and the man is sterile. If other cruel planets are in the house of death, they have come to grant death. || 63 ||**
If <term>Rahu: "Svarbhanu"</term> and <term>Saturn: "Bhanuja"</term> are in the eighth house <note>the house of death and longevity</note>, the woman will be barren and the man will be sterile or <note>impotent: "napunsaka"</note>. If other malefic planets are in the eighth house, they signify a time of death. || 63 ||

**The Sun destroys the one born before, Saturn destroys the one born after; Mars destroys every child born when situated in the house of siblings. || 64 ||**
If the Sun is in the third house, it destroys the elder brother. If Saturn is there, it destroys the younger brother. If Mars is situated in the <term>house of siblings: "Sahaja-sthana"</term>, it destroys all brothers who are born. || 64 ||

->Lagnachandrika (Moonlight of the Ascendant), Aries Ascendant, Royal Alignment, Jupiter, Barren, Sterile, Saturn, Misfortune, Trine, Angle<-

<page-num>21</page-num>
<header>Moonlight of the Ascendant with Commentary</header>

### With Commentary

**When gentle planets occupy the four angles and cruel planets are in the twelfth and sixth houses, the person becomes a world-renowned king, decorated and possessing the royal parasol. || 65 ||**
If <term>benefic planets: "Saumya-graha"</term> <note>such as Jupiter, Venus, Mercury, or a strong Moon</note> are in the four <term>angles: "Kendra"</term> (the 1st, 4th, 7th, and 10th houses), and <term>malefic planets: "Papa-graha"</term> are in the twelfth and sixth houses, then the person becomes a famous <term>Chhatrapati: "Lord of the Parasol"</term> <note>a title for a sovereign king</note> and is highly honored. || 65 ||

**When the Sun and Jupiter are in the fifth house from the Ascendant, the person born becomes an excellent man, full of knowledge and wealth. || 66 ||**
If the <term>Sun: "Surya"</term> and <term>Jupiter: "Brihaspati"</term> are positioned in the fifth house from the <term>Ascendant: "Lagna"</term>, the individual will be an outstanding person, abundant in both education and riches. || 66 ||

**If even one among Mercury, Jupiter, or a strong Venus is in an angle, the child born then is wealthy and an expert in the Vedas. || 67 ||**
If any one of these—<term>Mercury: "Budha"</term>, Jupiter, or a powerful <term>Venus: "Bhrigu"</term>—is situated in one of the angles (the 1st, 4th, 7th, or 10th houses), the child born will be very wealthy and a <term>Vedaparaga: "Master of the Vedas"</term> <note>one who has "crossed over" or fully mastered sacred knowledge</note>. || 67 ||

**If two or three gentle planets are in their signs of debilitation, or are in the twelfth house, the sixth, or the eighth, even wealthy men become beggars. || 68 ||**
If two or three benefic planets are in their <term>sign of debilitation: "Neecha Rashi"</term> <note>the zodiac sign where a planet is at its weakest</note>, or if they are placed in the twelfth house, or in the sixth or <term>eighth: "Nidhana"</term> houses, then even those born into wealth will become <term>bhikshukas: "beggars"</term>. || 68 ||

**If a planet is in its sign of debilitation at birth, but the ruler of that sign or the ruler of its sign of exaltation is in a trine or an angle, the person will be a righteous universal emperor. || 69 ||**
When a planet is debilitated at the time of birth, but the lord of that debilitated sign (or the lord of the sign where that planet would be exalted) is placed in a <term>trine: "Trikona"</term> (the 5th or 9th houses) or an angle (1st, 4th, 7th, or 10th), the person becomes a <term>Chakravarti: "Universal Monarch"</term> who is deeply devoted to <term>Dharma: "Righteousness"</term>. <note>This describes a famous astrological concept called "Neecha-bhanga Rajayoga," where a planetary weakness is cancelled and transformed into a great strength.</note> || 69 ||

**A person born with a cruel planet in the sixth house is a destroyer of enemy factions. With a gentle planet in the sixth, one is always sickly. A Moon in the sixth house brings death. || 70 ||**
If a <term>malefic planet: "Krura graha"</term> is in the sixth house, the person will crush their enemies. If a benefic planet is in the sixth house, they will suffer from constant illness. If the <term>Moon: "Chandra"</term> is in the sixth house, it leads to <term>Mrityuda: "The Giver of Death"</term> <note>often interpreted as a shortened lifespan or severe health crises in infancy</note>. || 70 ||

<vocab>Four angles (Chatuh-kendra), Gentle planets (Saumya), Cruel planets (Papa), World-renowned king (Raja-vikhyata), Possessing the parasol (Labdha-chhatra), Ascendant (Lagna), Fifth house (Panchama-sthana), Sun and Jupiter (Surya-Brihaspati), Wealthy (Dhanadhya), Master of the Vedas (Veda-paraga), Sign of debilitation (Neecha Rashi), Beggar (Bhikshuka), Trine (Trikona), Universal Emperor (Chakravarti), Malefic planet (Krura-graha), Destroyer of enemies (Shatru-paksha-vimardaka), Death-bringing (Mrityuda)</vocab>

<summary>This page outlines planetary positions that result in either royal status and Vedic scholarship or poverty and chronic illness. It highlights the significance of the sixth house for health and enemies, and explains the "Neecha-bhanga" yoga where debilitated planets can lead to imperial power under certain conditions.</summary>
<keywords>Rajayoga, Debilitation, Sixth House, Wealth, Vedic Scholarship, Royalty, Health, Neecha-bhanga, Planetary Alignment</keywords>

<meta>This page continues the "Lagnachandrika," specifically focusing on the results of various planets occupying the third house from the Ascendant. While the previous page discussed royal status and general prosperity, this section shifts toward family matters, specifically the number of children and siblings, as well as physical appearance.</meta>

<page-num>22</page-num>
<header>Moonlight of the Ascendant</header>

**If the Son of the Moon is located in the third house from the Ascendant, two sons and three daughters are born; of this there is no doubt. || 71 ||**

If <term>Mercury: "Budha"</term> <note>original: "Chandrasuta," literally "Son of the Moon" in Sanskrit</note> is seated in the third house from the <term>Ascendant: "Lagna"</term>, that person will have two sons and three daughters—there is no uncertainty regarding this. || 71 ||

---

**When the powerful Lord of Speech is in the third house from the Ascendant, then five sons are born to that man indeed. || 72 ||**

If a strong <term>Jupiter: "Brihaspati"</term> <note>original: "Vachaspati," an epithet for Jupiter meaning "Lord of Speech"</note> is in the third house from the Ascendant, that person will have five sons. || 72 ||

---

**When Saturn and the Moon are situated in the third house from the Ascendant, the child is born with a dark complexion and is deprived of brothers. || 73 ||**

If <term>Saturn: "Shani"</term> and the <term>Moon: "Chandra"</term> are located in the third house from the Ascendant, the child will have a <term>dark complexion: "Shyama-varna"</term> <note>literally "dusky-colored," often compared to the color of a rain cloud</note> and will be without brothers. || 73 ||

---

**When powerful Venus is in the third house from the Ascendant, two daughters and three sons are certainly born to him. || 74 ||**

If a strong <term>Venus: "Shukra"</term> is in the third house from the Ascendant, know it as a certainty that there will be two daughters and three sons. || 74 ||

---

**When the Moon is joined with Rahu in the third house from the Ascendant, the child becomes brotherless but is also born wealthy. || 75 ||**

If the <term>Moon: "Shashi"</term> is joined with <term>Rahu</term> <note>the north node of the Moon, a shadow planet associated with worldly desire and sudden changes</note> in the third house from the Ascendant, the child will have no brothers but will be <term>wealthy: "Lakshmivan"</term> <note>meaning "possessing Lakshmi," the goddess of fortune and prosperity</note>. || 75 ||

---

**If the Son of the Earth is in the third or the fifth house, that person—whether a woman or a man—dies from the grief of a son. || 76 ||**

If <term>Mars: "Mangal"</term> <note>original: "Dharasuta," literally "Son of the Earth"</note> is in the third or the fifth house, the person (whether a woman or a man) will suffer the <term>sorrow of a son: "Putra-duhkha"</term> <note>traditionally referring to the grief of losing a child or suffering because of them</note> at the time of death. || 76 ||

<summary>This page details the specific effects of planets like Mercury, Jupiter, Saturn, Venus, and Mars when positioned in the third house, primarily predicting the number of children and the presence of siblings.</summary>

<meta>This page transitions from the results of planets in the third house to the effects of planets in the seventh house, specific astrological conditions for physical health—particularly eyesight—and the prestigious "Rajayoga" or royal combinations. It continues the systematic analysis of how planetary positions at the moment of birth shape a person's life path, family, and profession.</meta>

<page-num>23</page-num>
<header>With Commentary in the Common Language</header>

## **When powerful Venus is located in the seventh house from the Ascendant, there are indeed two daughters, three sons, and the person becomes wealthy. || 77 ||**

If a strong <term>Venus: "Shukra"</term> is seated in the seventh house from the <term>Ascendant: "Lagna"</term> <note>the sign rising on the eastern horizon at birth; the 7th house typically represents marriage and partnerships</note>, the person will have two daughters and three sons, and will be a man of great wealth. || 77 ||

---

## **When Venus or even Saturn is situated in the Ascendant of Leo, the destruction of the eyes occurs for the child born there. || 78 ||**

If the <term>Ascendant: "Lagna"</term> is <term>Leo: "Simha"</term> and either <term>Venus: "Shukra"</term> or <term>Saturn: "Shani"</term> is placed there, the child born under this combination will suffer from the loss of their eyesight. || 78 ||

---

## **The Sun in the eighth, the Moon in the sixth, Mars in the second, and Saturn in the twelfth—due to the defect of these planets, they produce blindness in the eyes. || 79 ||**

If the <term>Sun: "Surya"</term> is in the eighth house, the <term>Moon: "Chandra"</term> is in the sixth house, <term>Mars: "Mangala"</term> is in the second house, and <term>Saturn: "Shani"</term> is in the twelfth house, this specific arrangement of planetary faults causes that man to be blind. || 79 ||

---

## **Birth in a beneficial Vargottama, with beneficial planets in the place of expenditure, with non-empty Kendras and "Significator" planets, this is called Rajayoga. || 80 ||**

If a person is born in a beneficial <term>Vargottama: "best division"</term> <note>a planet is Vargottama when it occupies the same zodiac sign in both the main birth chart and the important "Navamsha" divisional chart, greatly increasing its strength</note>, and if there are beneficial planets in the <term>twelfth house: "Vyaya-sthana"</term> <note>literally the "place of expenditure"</note>, and if the <term>Kendras: "centers"</term> <note>the 1st, 4th, 7th, and 10th houses, which act as the pillars of a horoscope</note> are "non-empty" (meaning they are filled with planets), and "Significator" <note>original: "Karaka," planets with special power to produce results</note> planets are present, then this combination is known as <term>Rajayoga: "Union of Kings"</term> <note>a combination indicating high status, power, or royal-like success</note>. || 80 ||

---

## **With the Sun in a Kendra, one serves the king; with the Moon, one follows the profession of a merchant; with Mars, one is a hero following the profession of arms; and with Mercury, one becomes a teacher. || 81 ||**
## **With Jupiter, a man is daily devoted to his own religious duties and possesses divine intellect; with Venus, he is endowed with learning and wealth; and with Saturn, he serves those of low status. || 82 ||**

If the <term>Sun: "Surya"</term> is in a <term>Kendra</term> <note>one of the four central houses</note>, the person will be a servant of the king (such as a minister). If the <term>Moon: "Nishakara"</term> <note>original: "Nishakara," literally "the Night-maker"</note> is in a Kendra, he follows the trade of a merchant. If <term>Mars: "Kuja"</term> <note>original: "Kuja," literally "Born of the Earth"</note> is there, he will be a brave warrior who lives by the profession of weapons. If <term>Mercury: "Budha"</term> is there, the boy will grow up to be a teacher. If <term>Jupiter: "Brihaspati"</term> <note>also referred to as "Guru"</note> is there, the man will be constantly devoted to his religious rituals and will possess a divine or exceptionally brilliant intellect. If <term>Venus: "Shukra"</term> is there, he will be blessed with education and wealth. If <term>Saturn: "Shanaishchara"</term> is there, he will be one who serves those of lower social standing.

<vocab>Ascendant (Lagna), Venus (Shukra), Saturn (Shani), Sun (Surya), Moon (Chandra), Mars (Mangala), Mercury (Budha), Jupiter (Brihaspati), Centers (Kendra), Royal Combination (Rajayoga), Best Division (Vargottama), Night-maker (Nishakara), Born of the Earth (Kuja), Servant of the Lowly (Nichasevi)</vocab>

<meta>This page continues the specific planetary combinations (yogas) from the "Lagnachandrika." It transitions from general character traits of those performing rituals to specific, often dire, predictions concerning life expectancy, physical infirmities like blindness, and the loss of siblings based on planetary alignments in specific houses.</meta>

<header>Moonlight of the Ascendant</header>

...[Such a person] performs sacrifices and rituals, and is endowed with divine intellect. If Venus is present, they possess knowledge and wealth, and if Saturn is present, they serve those of low status. || 81-82 ||

---

->**If Rahu is joined with Mars, Venus, and Mercury in the house of action (the tenth) or in the Ascendant,**<-
->**his growth and decline occur in an instant. || 83 ||**<-

If <term>Rahu: "the North Node of the Moon"</term> is placed in the <term>tenth house: "karmasthana," the house of profession</term> or in the <term>Ascendant: "Lagna"</term> along with <term>Mars: "Mangala"</term>, <term>Venus: "Shukra"</term>, and <term>Mercury: "Budha"</term>, then that man rises in one moment and falls in the next—meaning he fluctuates constantly between happiness and sorrow. || 83 ||

---

->**If the Sun is situated in the twelfth sign of the birth chart,**<-
->**it causes blindness in the right eye; the Moon [does so] in the left eye. || 84 ||**<-

In the birth chart, if the <term>Sun: "Divakara"</term> <note>literally "the day-maker"</note> is in the twelfth house from the Ascendant, the person will be blind in the right eye; if the <term>Moon: "Chandrama"</term> is there, they will be blind in the left eye. || 84 ||

---

->**When Jupiter is in the sign of Mars and the Son of Earth (Mars) is in the sign of Jupiter,**<-
->**the death of the child occurs in the twelfth year; of this there is no doubt. || 85 ||**<-

If <term>Jupiter: "Brihaspati"</term> is in a sign ruled by <term>Mars: "Mangala"</term> <note>Aries or Scorpio</note> and Mars is in a sign ruled by Jupiter <note>Sagittarius or Pisces</note>, then the death of that child will certainly occur in their twelfth year. || 85 ||

---

->**When Mars is in the house of wealth accompanied by Saturn,**<-
->**and Rahu is in the house of siblings, his brother does not survive. || 86 ||**<-

If <term>Mars: "Mangala"</term> joined with <term>Saturn: "Shanaishchara"</term> <note>literally "the slow-mover"</note> is in the <term>second house: "dhanasthana"</term> and <term>Rahu</term> is in the <term>third house: "sahaja," the house of siblings</term>, then his brothers do not live. || 86 ||

---

->**When Rahu is in the fourth and the Moon is in the sixth or eighth,**<-
->**death occurs immediately, even if Shiva himself protects him. || 87 ||**<-

If <term>Rahu</term> is in the fourth house and the <term>Moon: "Chandra"</term> is in the sixth or eighth house, death follows quickly; even if <term>Lord Shiva: "Shankara"</term> were to offer protection, the person would not survive. || 87 ||

---

->**The Lord of Night (the Moon) situated in the eighth, joined with a malefic in a cardinal house,**<-
->**and when Rahu is in the fourth, he lives for one year. || 88 ||**<-

<note>This verse describes a "Balarishta" or a combination leading to infant mortality. The Moon is called "Nishanatha," the Lord of the Night. A cardinal house or "Kendra" refers to the 1st, 4th, 7th, or 10th houses.</note>

---

If the <term>Moon: "Chandrama"</term> is in the eighth house, a <term>malefic planet: "papagraha"</term> is in an angle <note>the 1st, 4th, 7th, or 10th houses</note>, and <term>Rahu: "the North Node"</term> is in the fourth house, the child does not live even for one year. || 88 ||

---

->**If a malefic is situated in the underworld, the sky, and the twelfth house,**<-
->**it destroys the father and mother, or the person wanders from country to country. || 89 ||**<-

If malefic planets are situated in the <term>fourth house: "patala" (the underworld)</term>, the <term>tenth house: "ambara" (the sky)</term>, and the twelfth house, then the person's parents will be lost, or the person will leave their own country to go abroad. || 89 ||

---

->**The Lord of the Night in the fifth, Jupiter in a trine,**<-
->**and the Son of Earth in the tenth; such a one lives a full life. || 90 ||**<-

If the <term>Moon: "Nishanatha" (Lord of the Night)</term> is in the fifth house, <term>Jupiter: "Brihaspati"</term> is in a trine <note>the 5th or 9th houses</note>, and <term>Mars: "Mahisunu" (Son of the Earth)</term> is in the tenth house, the person is blessed with a very long life. || 90 ||

---

->**When Venus is in the house of wealth joined with a cruel planet,**<-
->**and does not look upon its own sign, then there are few sons. || 91 ||**<-

If <term>Venus: "Shukra"</term> is in the <term>second house: "dhanasthana"</term> along with a <term>malefic planet: "kruragraha"</term> and does not aspect <note>astrologically "glance at"</note> its own zodiac sign, then the person will have very few sons. || 91 ||

---

->**When a cruel planet is in the house of wealth, and likewise in the third and seventh,**<-
->**and Jupiter is in the fifth house, one is then of low birth. || 92 ||**<-

If a malefic planet is in the second house, and malefic planets are also in the <term>third: "sahaja"</term> and <term>seventh: "saptama"</term> houses, while <term>Jupiter: "Jiva"</term> is in the fifth house, it should be said that the person was born from the seed of one of low status. || 92 ||

---

->**When planets are in the ninth, the fifth, and the fourth houses,**<-
->**the first-born is destroyed while the one born after lives. || 93 ||**<-

If <term>malefic planets</term> <note>clarified by the commentary</note> are in the ninth, fifth, and fourth houses, then the child born first will die, and the child born later will survive. || 93 ||

---

->**From another married woman, there will be one son;**<-
->**he will be famous in the world, a renouncer, long-lived, and of great intellect. || 94 ||**<-

->**If a cruel planet is born in the ascendant, the house of wealth, the house of death, or the house of loss,**<-
->**the child’s passage is blocked by waste, and they perish on the twelfth or eighth day. || 85 ||**<-

If <term>malefic planets: "papagraha"</term> are situated in the first (ascendant), second, eighth, or twelfth houses, the bowels become obstructed, and the child may pass away on the twelfth or eighth day of life. || 95 ||

->**and the Son of Saturn is in the eighth, his wife does not survive. || 86 ||**<-

->**At the end of a lunar day, the end of a solar day, the end of an ascendant, or the end of a constellation,**<-
->**or if born in a movable subdivision, the infant is born of another. || 87 ||**<-

One should know that a child born at the very end of a <term>lunar day: "tithi"</term>, the end of the day, the end of an <term>ascendant: "lagna"</term>, the end of a <term>lunar mansion: "nakshatra"</term>, or within a <term>movable subdivision: "chara navamsha"</term>, is born from someone other than the father. || 97 ||

If Mars sits in its own zodiac sign and <note>astrologically</note> aspects the <term>tenth house: "karmasthana" (the house of profession)</term>, and is joined with Mercury and Venus, the person will receive very little reward or result for their hard work. || 98 ||

If the Moon is in the <term>sixth house: "ripusthana" (the house of enemies)</term>, Saturn is in the first house (ascendant), and Mars is in the seventh house, the person's father will not live. || 99 ||

## Including the Language Commentary

->**When a cruel planet is in the eleventh house, and the Moon and Venus are in the fifth,**<-
->**the first-born is a daughter and the mother undergoes great suffering. || 100 ||**<-

If a <term>malefic planet: "krura graha"</term> <note>original: "क्रूर ग्रह"</note> is in the eleventh house, and the Moon and <term>Venus: "Bhargava"</term> are in the fifth house, then that man’s first child will be a girl and his mother will endure much hardship. || 100 ||

---

->**When Rahu is situated in the third house and Jupiter is in the house of wealth,**<-
->**joined by Mercury, he shall have three brothers. || 101 ||**<-

If <term>Rahu: "the North Node"</term> is in the third house, and <term>Jupiter: "Brihaspati"</term> is in the second house <note>the house of wealth</note> along with <term>Mercury: "Budha"</term>, he will have three brothers. || 101 ||

---

->**If the Sun is in Sagittarius, Saturn in Aquarius, and the Moon in Aries;**<-
->**when Venus is in Capricorn, he does not enjoy his father’s wealth at all. || 102 ||**<-

If the Sun is in <term>Sagittarius: "Chapa"</term>, Saturn is in Aquarius, the Moon is in Aries, and Venus is in Capricorn, the person will not benefit from or enjoy his father's inheritance. || 102 ||

---

->**Cruel planets in the four centers, or a cruel planet in the house of wealth;**<-
->**one should recognize this as the "poverty combination," the destroyer of one’s own lineage. || 103 ||**<-

If there are malefic planets in all four <term>cardinal houses: "kendras" (1st, 4th, 7th, and 10th)</term> as well as in the second house, this "poverty combination" <note>original: "daridryayogam"</note> leads to the ruin of the family line. || 103 ||

---

->**When Jupiter is situated in the ascendant and Saturn is in the house of wealth,**<-
->**and Rahu is in the third house, his mother does not live. || 104 ||**<-

If Jupiter is in the <term>ascendant: "lagna"</term>, Saturn is in the second house, and Rahu is in the third house, his mother will not survive. || 104 ||

---

->**When Mars is in the seventh house and Venus is in the eighth,**<-
->**and the Sun is in the ninth house, he is born with a short life. || 105 ||**<-

If <term>Mars: "Mangala"</term> is in the seventh house, Venus is in the eighth, and the Sun is in the ninth house, that man will have a short lifespan. || 105 ||

# Lagnachandrika

->**When Rahu and Jupiter are in the house of enemies, the ascendant, or the fourth house;**<-
->**in the twenty-third year, that son shall cause the destruction of his father. || 106 ||**<-

If <term>Rahu: "the North Node"</term> and <term>Jupiter: "Brihaspati"</term> are located in the <term>sixth house: "ripu-kshetra" (the house of enemies)</term>, the first house <note>the ascendant</note>, or the fourth house, then in his twenty-third year, that son will bring about his father's end. || 106 ||

---

->**If at the time of a child’s birth, Saturn is situated in the eighth house,**<-
->**the child will become a leper and die within one month; of this, there is no doubt. || 107 ||**<-

If <term>Saturn: "Shanaishchara"</term> is in the eighth house <note>the house of longevity and death</note> at the moment of birth, the child will suffer from leprosy and will certainly pass away within a single month. || 107 ||

---

->**When Mercury is in the sixth or eighth house from the birth ascendant and is aspected by malefics,**<-
->**death occurs in the fourth year, even if Lord Shiva himself protects him. || 108 ||**<-

If <term>Mercury: "Budha"</term> is positioned in the sixth or eighth house from the birth <term>ascendant: "lagna"</term> and is under the influence of <term>malefic planets: "krura graha"</term> <note>planets like Mars or Saturn that are traditionally seen as harsh</note>, the child will die in their fourth year; this fate is unavoidable even through the divine intervention of <term>Shiva: "Shankara"</term>. || 108 ||

---

->**When Mars is in the eighth house and the Sun is debilitated in a trine,**<-
->**he shall quickly become a beggar and live in sorrow. || 109 ||**<-

If <term>Mars: "Bhauma"</term> is in the eighth house and the Sun is <term>debilitated: "neecha-ga"</term> <note>at its weakest point in the zodiac</note> while in a <term>trine: "trikona"</term> <note>the 5th or 9th houses</note>, the person will soon fall into a life of misery and sustain himself through begging. || 109 ||

---

->**Mars in Leo, Saturn in Libra, and Venus in Virgo;**<-
->**when Rahu is in Gemini, his mother shall perish. || 110 ||**<-

If Mars is in Leo, Saturn is in Libra, <term>Venus: "Sita"</term> is in Virgo, and Rahu is in Gemini, the child’s mother will not survive. || 110 ||

---

->**When a malefic in its own sign is in the ascendant, and a malefic is in the fourth house,**<-
->**and another malefic is in the tenth house, the child lives a life of great hardship. || 111 ||**<-

If a malefic planet is situated in its own zodiac sign within the first house <note>the ascendant</note>, and there are also malefics in the <term>fourth house: "patala"</term> and the tenth house, that child will survive only with great difficulty and suffering. || 111 ||
