# Lagnachandrika
### With Vernacular Commentary

->**If the Lord of the Night is in the eighth house, a malefic is in a cardinal house,**<-
->**and Rahu is in the fourth house, the child lives for only one year. || 112 ||**<-

->**If the child survives due to the aspect of auspicious planets,**<-
->**he will possess a wicked nature and perform cruel deeds. || 113 ||**<-

If the <term>Moon: "Nishanatha" (Lord of the Night)</term> is in the eighth house, a <term>malefic planet: "krura graha"</term> is in a <term>cardinal house: "kendra"</term> <note>the 1st, 4th, 7th, or 10th houses, which are the pillars of a birth chart</note>, and <term>Rahu: "the North Node"</term> is in the fourth house, then that child will live for only one year. If the child survives because of the <term>aspect: "drishti"</term> <note>the "glance" or mathematical influence of one planet upon another</note> of <term>benefic planets: "shubha graha"</term> <note>such as Jupiter or Venus</note>, he will live, but he will be of a wicked disposition and a worker of cruel deeds. || 112–113 ||

---

->**Moon in the ascendant, Venus in the second, Mercury and Sun in the twelfth,**<-
->**and Rahu in the fifth: such a child will be a perpetrator of violence and imprisonment. || 114 ||**<-

If the Moon is in the <term>ascendant: "lagna"</term>, <term>Venus: "Shukra"</term> is in the second house, <term>Mercury: "Budha"</term> and the <term>Sun: "Bhaskara"</term> are in the twelfth house, and Rahu is in the fifth house, then that child will grow to be one who commits acts of violence and causes the imprisonment of others. || 114 ||

---

->**Sun in the seventh house, the Son of Earth in the tenth,**<-
->**and Rahu in the twelfth: his father lives with great difficulty. || 115 ||**<-

If the <term>Sun: "Bhanu"</term> is in the seventh house, the <term>Son of Earth: "Bhuminandana" (Mars)</term> is in the tenth house <note>the house of career and actions</note>, and Rahu is in the twelfth house, then the father of that child will lead a life filled with suffering and hardship. || 115 ||

---

->**When malefic planets are positioned in the seventh, twelfth, third, or tenth houses,**<-
->**then suffering in the body of the newborn child should be predicted. || 116 ||**<-

If malefic planets are situated in the seventh, twelfth, third, or tenth houses, it indicates that the newborn child will suffer from physical ailments and bodily pain. || 116 ||

---

->**When Rahu, Venus, Mars, and Saturn are all in Virgo,**<-
->**the person born there possesses wealth greater than that of Kubera. || 117 ||**<-

If the planets Rahu, Venus, <term>Mars: "Bhauma"</term>, and <term>Saturn: "Shani"</term> are all located in the zodiac sign of <term>Virgo: "Kanya"</term>, the person born under this configuration will be more <term>wealthy: "Lakshmivan"</term> <note>literally "possessing the grace of Lakshmi, the goddess of fortune"</note> than even <term>Kubera: "the treasurer of the gods"</term>. || 117 ||

# Lagnachandrika
### With Vernacular Commentary

->**If Rahu, Mercury, Venus, Saturn, and the Sun are located in the second house,**<-
->**the mother dies, and the father has already passed away. || 118 ||**<-

If <term>Rahu: "the North Node"</term>, <term>Mercury: "Budha"</term>, <term>Venus: "Shukra"</term>, <term>Mars: "Mangal"</term> <note>original: "मङ्गल" — the Hindi commentary includes Mars in this list, though it is omitted in the Sanskrit verse</note>, <term>Saturn: "Sauri"</term>, and the <term>Sun: "Surya"</term> are positioned in the second house <note>the house traditionally associated with family and accumulated wealth</note>, then the father dies before the child is born, and the mother dies shortly thereafter. || 118 ||

---

->**If the Sun, Rahu, Saturn, Mercury, and Jupiter are in the ascendant or the fifth house,**<-
->**the child born under this combination perishes immediately. || 119 ||**<-

If the Sun, Rahu, <term>Saturn: "Shani"</term>, Mercury, and <term>Jupiter: "Brihaspati"</term> are located in the <term>ascendant: "lagna"</term> <note>the first house, representing the physical body and the self</note> or the fifth house <note>the house of children and intelligence</note>, the child born under this <term>yoga: "astrological combination"</term> will die at the very moment of birth. || 119 ||

---

->**If Jupiter, the Sun, Rahu, and Mars fall within the malefic divisions,**<-
->**and Venus is in the seventh house, the body will always suffer. || 120 ||**<-

If the four planets Jupiter, the Sun, Rahu, and Mars are positioned within the <term>malefic shadvarga: "the six-fold divisional charts of cruel planets"</term> <note>original: "क्रूर ग्रह के षड्वर्ग" — a complex calculation where a planet's strength is assessed across six sub-charts; here, they fall into signs ruled by "cruel" planets like Saturn or Mars</note>, and Venus is placed in the seventh house, the individual will suffer from constant physical pain or chronic health struggles throughout their life. || 120 ||

---

->**If one is born in a malefic ascendant and its lord is joined with malefics,**<-
->**rheumatism will cause great physical distress. || 121 ||**<-

If the birth occurs when the <term>ascendant: "lagna"</term> is a <term>malefic sign: "krura rashi"</term> <note>signs ruled by Mars or Saturn, such as Aries, Scorpio, Capricorn, or Aquarius</note>, and the lord of that ascendant is seated with other <term>malefic planets: "krura grahas"</term>, the person will be afflicted by <term>rheumatism: "amavata"</term> <note>original: "आमवात" — a traditional Ayurvedic term for joint pain and digestive toxins</note> and will experience significant physical hardship. || 121 ||

---

->**If Mars, Rahu, and Saturn are together in the seventh house,**<-
->**one suffers from the king and never sits upon his own seat. || 122 ||**<-

If Mars, Rahu, and Saturn are all seated together in the <term>seventh house: "saptam ghar"</term> <note>the Sanskrit verse calls this the "guhyasthana" or secret place, which can refer to the reproductive organs or private life</note>, the person will face <term>persecution by the king: "nripapida"</term> <note>original: "नृपपीडा" — in modern terms, this refers to legal troubles, government fines, or harassment by authorities</note> and will never be stable or secure in his own position or status. || 122 ||

---

->**If the lord of the third house is in the third, first, fifth, or second house,**<-
->**the child born at that time will not survive. || 123 ||**<-

If the lord of the <term>third house: "sahaje"</term> <note>the house of siblings and courage</note> is situated in either the third, the ascendant, the fifth, or the second house, a child born during that time will not live long. || 123 ||

# With Vernacular Commentary

->**If Rahu is in Virgo or Gemini, and located in an angular house, the sixth house, the twelfth house, or a trine, the person born will be a giver, an enjoyer, and free from disease. || 124 ||**<-

If <term>Rahu: "the North Node"</term> is positioned in the signs of <term>Virgo: "Kanya"</term> or <term>Gemini: "Mithuna"</term>, and is placed in a <term>Kendra: "angular house (1st, 4th, 7th, or 10th)"</term>, the <term>sixth house: "shastha"</term>, the <term>twelfth house: "vyaya"</term>, or a <term>Trikona: "trine (5th or 9th house)"</term>, the individual born under this combination will be generous, will enjoy life's pleasures, and will remain healthy. || 124 ||

---

->**If Rahu, Saturn, and the Sun are in the fourth house, and the Moon, Mercury, Mars, and Venus are in the sixth house, the person born will cause the ruin of their own home. || 125 ||**<-

When <term>Rahu</term>, <term>Saturn: "Sauri"</term>, and the <term>Sun: "Arka"</term> occupy the fourth house <note>the house traditionally associated with home, mother, and land</note>, while the <term>Moon: "Chandra"</term>, <term>Mercury: "Budha"</term>, <term>Mars: "Kuja"</term>, and <term>Venus: "Bhargava"</term> are in the sixth house, the person born under this alignment becomes a destroyer of their own household and family lineage. || 125 ||

---

->**If a single malefic planet is in the eighth house in an enemy's sign and is aspected by another malefic, the child will die within a year. || 126 ||**<-

If even one <term>malefic planet: "paapa-graha"</term> <note>such as Mars, Saturn, Rahu, or Ketu</note> is situated in the <term>eighth house: "ashtama sthana"</term> <note>the house of longevity and death</note> in the sign of its astrological enemy, and is further <term>aspected: "viikshito"</term> <note>glanced upon by the influence of another planet</note> by another malefic planet, the child born will perish within one year. || 126 ||

---

->**If Mars, the Sun, and Saturn are in their enemy's signs in the eighth house, even if protected by Lord Shiva, the child will not live for more than a year. || 127 ||**<-

If <term>Mars: "Bhauma"</term>, the <term>Sun: "Bhaskara"</term>, and <term>Saturn: "Manda"</term> are all placed in the eighth house in signs belonging to their enemies, the child will not survive beyond a single year, even if <term>Lord Shiva: "the deity of immortality"</term> himself were to offer protection. || 127 ||

---

->**If retrograde Saturn is in a sign of Mars and occupies an angular house, the sixth, or the eighth house, and is aspected by a strong Mars, the infant dies in two years. || 128 ||**<-

When a <term>retrograde: "vaktri"</term> <note>appearing to move backward in the sky</note> Saturn is in a sign ruled by Mars <note>Aries or Scorpio</note> and is located in a <term>Kendra</term>, the sixth, or the eighth house, and is aspected by a powerful Mars, the child born will die within two years. || 128 ||

---

->**If Rahu is in Taurus and aspected by three planets, or if Ketu is in the four angular houses aspected by Jupiter and Venus, the person lives for a long time. || 129 ||**<-

<note>This verse lists the counter-conditions where malefic placements are mitigated to grant long life.</note>

# Moonlight of Ascendants

If <term>Rahu: "the North Node of the Moon"</term> is in the sign of <term>Taurus: "Vrisha"</term> and is aspected <note>viewed by the astrological "glance" of a planet</note> by three planets, or is aspected by <term>Jupiter: "Brihaspati"</term> and <term>Venus: "Shukra"</term>, the person born lives for a long time. || 129 ||

->**When a conjunction of the Moon and Mars occurs at the time of birth,**<-
->**the Goddess of Fortune never leaves the home of that person. || 130 ||**<-

If the <term>Moon: "Chandra"</term> and <term>Mars: "Mangala"</term> are joined together <note>this specific alignment is known as Chandra-Mangala Yoga</note> at the time of an individual's birth, <term>Lakshmi: "the Goddess of Wealth"</term> never departs from that child’s household. || 130 ||

->**Saturn, aspected by a malefic in an angular house, kills in sixteen days.**<-
->**Mars kills in six months, and the Sun kills in one year. || 131 ||**<-

If <term>Saturn: "Shani"</term> is situated in a <term>Kendra: "an angular house (1st, 4th, 7th, or 10th)"</term> and is aspected by a <term>malefic planet: "paapa-graha"</term>, it causes death within sixteen days; if <term>Mars</term> is in such a position, it causes death in six months; and if the <term>Sun: "Surya"</term> is there, it causes death within one year. || 131 ||

->**The Moon in the sixth or eighth house, aspected by a malefic, leads to immediate death.**<-
->**If aspected by a benefic, death occurs in the eighth year; if by both, in half that time. || 132 ||**<-

If the Moon is positioned in the <term>sixth: "shastha"</term> or <term>eighth house: "ashtama"</term> <note>these are considered difficult or "dusthana" houses</note> and is aspected by a malefic planet, it causes death very quickly. If it is aspected by <term>benefic planets: "shubha-graha"</term> <note>such as Jupiter, Venus, or Mercury</note>, death occurs in the eighth year. If it is aspected by a mixture of both benefic and malefic planets, death occurs in the fourth year <note>half of eight</note>. || 132 ||

->**Born in the waxing phase at night, or in the waning phase during the day,**<-
->**the Moon in the sixth or eighth house does not kill, but protects like a father. || 133 ||**<-

If a birth occurs during the <term>waxing phase: "Shukla Paksha"</term> at night, or during the <term>waning phase: "Krishna Paksha"</term> during the day, even if the Moon is placed in the sixth or eighth house, it does not harm the child. Instead, it protects the infant just as a father protects his child. || 133 ||

->**The Moon joined with Saturn, Rahu, and Mars in the seventh or ninth house,**<-
->**kills the infant on the seventh day or in the seventh month. || 134 ||**<-

If the Moon is joined by Saturn, Rahu, and Mars in the <term>seventh house: "saptama"</term> or <term>ninth house: "navama"</term>, the child may pass away on the seventh day or in the seventh month after birth. || 134 ||

<vocab>Moonlight of Ascendants (Lagnachandrika), North Node (Rahu), Jupiter (Brihaspati), Venus (Shukra), Moon-Mars Conjunction (Chandra-Mangala Samyoga), Goddess of Wealth (Lakshmi), Saturn (Shani), Malefic Planet (Paapa-graha), Angular House (Kendra), Mars (Mangala), Sun (Surya), Sixth and Eighth Houses (Shashta-Ashtama), Waxing Phase (Shukla Paksha), Waning Phase (Krishna Paksha), Moon (Chandra)</vocab>

<header>With Vernacular Commentary</header>

# With Vernacular Commentary

**If the Moon is in the fifth, seventh, ascendant, or twelfth house, conjoined with a malefic planet, and is not aspected by strong benefic planets, it kills the infant. || 135 ||**
If the <term>Moon: "Chandra"</term> is positioned in the <term>fifth: "putra"</term>, <term>seventh: "kalatra"</term>, <term>ascendant: "lagna"</term>, or the <term>twelfth house: "vyaya"</term>, is joined with a <term>malefic planet: "papa-graha"</term>, and is not viewed by powerful <term>benefic planets: "shubha-graha"</term>, then it causes the death of the child. || 135 ||

**If the Moon is conjoined with malefic planets, or if the Moon is situated between malefic planets, or if there is a malefic in the seventh house from the Moon, then the mother’s destruction occurs. || 136 ||**
If the Moon is in the company of malefic planets, or is hemmed in between malefic planets <note>a condition known as Papa-kartari Yoga</note>, or if a malefic planet is in the seventh house from the Moon, his mother may perish. || 136 ||

**If the Sun is seated in the ascendant, the Moon is in the fifth house, and other malefic planets are in the eighth house, the child born does not live. || 137 ||**
When the <term>Sun: "Bhanu"</term> sits in the first house (ascendant), the <term>Moon: "Nishakara"</term> is in the fifth house, and other malefic planets are in the <term>eighth house: "ashtama"</term> <note>the house of longevity and death</note>, the newborn child will not survive. || 137 ||

**If the ascendant is conjoined with a malefic planet, or the ascendant is situated between malefic planets, or if malefic planets are in the seventh house from the ascendant, the mother's death occurs. || 138 ||**
If the ascendant is joined by a malefic planet, or if the ascendant is caught between two malefic planets, or if malefic planets are in the seventh house from the ascendant, it results in the death of the mother. || 138 ||

**If the Sun is conjoined with a malefic planet, or the Sun is situated between malefic planets, or if malefic planets are in the seventh house from the Sun, the father's death occurs. || 139 ||**
If the Sun is joined with a malefic planet, or is hemmed in between malefic planets, or if there are malefic planets in the seventh house from the Sun, it results in the death of the father. || 139 ||

**If birth occurs in the sign of a cruel planet and the lord of the ascendant is combust, the person born will perform ill deeds and live for only seven years. || 140 ||**
If the birth takes place in the <term>sign of a cruel planet: "krura-kshetra"</term> <note>referring to signs ruled by Mars or Saturn</note> and the <term>lord of the ascendant: "lagnesha"</term> is <term>combust: "astangata"</term> <note>rendered powerless by being too close to the Sun</note>, then the child will engage in improper actions and have a lifespan of only seven years. || 140 ||

<header>The Moonlight of the Ascendant</header>

**When Saturn is in the eighth house and the Moon is in the birth sign, the person is born with weak digestion, stomach ailments, and bodily defects. || 141 ||**
If <term>Saturn: "Sauri"</term> is in the eighth house <note>the house of longevity and chronic illness</note> and the <term>Moon: "Chandrama"</term> is in the birth <term>ascendant: "Lagna"</term>, that person will suffer from <term>weak digestion: "mandagni"</term> <note>literally "slow fire," a term for poor metabolic health</note>, stomach diseases, and have a deficient or impaired body. || 141 ||

**If the Sun is in Saturn's sign and Saturn is in the Sun's sign, the death of that newborn occurs in the twelfth year. || 142 ||**
When the <term>Sun: "Bhanu"</term> is located in a sign ruled by Saturn <note>Capricorn or Aquarius</note> and <term>Saturn: "Shani"</term> is in the sign ruled by the Sun <note>Leo</note>, the child born under this exchange of signs will die in their twelfth year. || 142 ||

**When Mercury and Mars are in the ascendant or situated in the sixth house, the person becomes a thief, a doer of terrible deeds, and their hands and feet are destroyed. || 143 ||**
If <term>Mercury: "Budha"</term> and <term>Mars: "Bhauma"</term> are in the first house or the <term>sixth house: "shastha-sthana"</term> <note>the house of debts, enemies, and accidents</note>, the child will become a thief and commit heinous acts, and his hands and feet may be ruined or lost. || 143 ||

**If Mercury is in the sixth, eighth, or first house and is in an enemy’s sign, there is no doubt the child will die in their fourth year. || 144 ||**
When Mercury is in the sixth or eighth house, or in the ascendant while situated in a sign belonging to its planetary enemy, the child’s death occurs in the fourth year; of this, there is no doubt. || 144 ||

**When Rahu is in the eighth house and the Moon is in an angular house, there is no doubt the child’s death occurs immediately. || 145 ||**
If <term>Rahu: "the North Node"</term> is in the eighth house and the Moon is in a <term>Kendra: "angular house"</term> <note>the 1st, 4th, 7th, or 10th houses, which are the pillars of the horoscope</note>, the infant's death happens very quickly. || 145 ||

**If Rahu is in the seventh house and in an enemy’s sign, there is no doubt that death occurs upon reaching the sixteenth year. || 146 ||**
When Rahu is in the seventh house and is placed in an enemy's sign, then upon reaching the sixteenth year, that person's death occurs without a doubt. || 146 ||

# Lagnachandrika

If one <term>malefic planet: "Papa-graha"</term> <note>Malefics include Mars, Saturn, Rahu, and Ketu</note> is seated in the <term>ascendant: "Lagna"</term> and another malefic is in the fourth house, that child shall be a <term>light to his lineage: "Kuldeepak"</term> <note>original: "Kuldeepak" — a metaphor for a child who brings great honor and glory to the family</note> and shall always remain happy. || 164 ||

> **If Mars is in the ascendant or the seventh house, and the Sun is in the fifth,**
> **he lives well even if left in the middle of a forest and becomes famous, without a doubt. || 165 ||**

If <term>Mars: "Bhauma"</term> is in the ascendant or the seventh house, and the <term>Sun: "Divakara"</term> is in the fifth house, even if the child were to be left abandoned in a forest, he would survive well and become famous. There is no doubt in this. || 165 ||

> **When one is born in the sign of Mars, and a malefic planet is in the ascendant,**
> **the death of the child occurs within a year, without a doubt. || 166 ||**

If the birth occurs in a sign ruled by Mars <note>Aries or Scorpio</note> and a <term>cruel planet: "Krura-graha"</term> is in the ascendant, that child will certainly die within one year. || 166 ||

> **A waning Moon in the twelfth house, aspected by malefics, brings sorrow;**
> **it causes great distress when Saturn is in the eighth house. || 167 ||**

If the <term>waning Moon: "Ksheena-chandra"</term> is in the twelfth house and is aspected <note>viewed by the astrological "glance" of a planet</note> by malefic planets, the life will be sorrowful; likewise, if <term>Saturn: "Shani"</term> is in the eighth house, it causes great affliction. || 167 ||

> **When the Moon is in the twelfth and a malefic is in the sixth house,**
> **the native is certainly short-lived and always sickly. || 168 ||**

If the Moon is in the twelfth house and a malefic planet is in the sixth house, that child will surely have a short lifespan and will always remain ill. || 168 ||

> **Rahu in the tenth house causes affliction to the mother and father;**
> **in the twelfth year, the death of that child is certain. || 169 ||**

If <term>Rahu: "the shadow planet"</term> is in the tenth house, it brings suffering to the parents, and the death of that child will surely occur in his twelfth year. || 169 ||

> **When the Sun is in the sign of Saturn, and Saturn is in the sign of the Sun,**
> **the death of the child occurs in the twentieth year, without a doubt. || 170 ||**

<note>This describes an exchange of houses (Parivartana): the Sun placed in Capricorn or Aquarius (Saturn's signs) while Saturn is placed in Leo (the Sun's sign).</note>

<meta>This page continues the discussion of "Arishta" or planetary afflictions. While the previous page focused on the child's own health and immediate survival, this section shifts toward specific ages of death (such as 20 years) and combinations that negatively impact the lifespan of the child's parents.</meta>

<page-num>39</page-num>
# With Vernacular Commentary

### With Vernacular Commentary

If the <term>Sun: "Surya"</term> is in the house of Saturn and <term>Saturn: "Shani"</term> is in the house of the Sun, know for certain that the death of that child will occur in their twentieth year. || 170 || <note>The "houses" refer to the zodiac signs ruled by these planets: the Sun rules Leo, while Saturn rules Capricorn and Aquarius. This refers to a "Parivartana" or mutual exchange of signs.</note>

> **If a malefic is in the house of enemies, the Moon is in the house of loss,**
> **and Mars is in the fourth house, his mother does not live. || 171 ||**

If a <term>malefic planet: "Papa-graha"</term> is in the sixth house <note>the "ripu-sthana" or house of enemies</note>, the <term>Moon: "Chandra"</term> is in the twelfth house <note>the "vyaya-sthana" or house of loss</note>, and <term>Mars: "Mangala"</term> is in the fourth house, his mother will not survive. || 171 ||

> **A malefic in the fourth house kills the mother; in the tenth, the father.**
> **A malefic in the seventh house destroys both parents. || 172 ||**

If a malefic planet is situated in the fourth house, it destroys <note>causes the death of</note> the mother; if a malefic is in the tenth house, it destroys the father; and if a malefic is in the seventh house, it destroys both parents. || 172 ||

> **When cruel planets are stationed in the twelfth or the house of enemies,**
> **know it as a danger to the mother; if in the fourth or tenth, to the father. || 173 ||**

If <term>cruel planets: "Krura-graha"</term> <note>malefics such as Saturn, Mars, or Rahu</note> are located in the twelfth or sixth houses, the mother will be destroyed; if a malefic is in the fourth or tenth house, the father will be destroyed. || 173 ||

> **Whether exalted or debilitated, if the Sun is in the seventh,**
> **the child born then quickly kills the mother; of this, there is no doubt. || 174 ||**

If the Sun, whether in its <term>sign of exaltation: "Uccha"</term> <note>Aries</note> or its <term>sign of debilitation: "Neecha"</term> <note>Libra</note>, is placed in the seventh house, the child born under this influence will cause the death of the mother. There is no doubt about this. || 174 ||

> **In the divisions of the Ram and others: eight, nine, twenty-four, twenty-two, five,**
> **one, four, two, twenty, eighteen, twenty-one, and ten; by these years, the Moon takes the life. || 175 ||**

This explains *when* the aforementioned combinations will cause the mother's death: depending on which <term>ninth-division: "Navamsha"</term> <note>a sub-division of a zodiac sign used for fine-tuning predictions</note> the Moon occupies, starting from Aries, death occurs in the following years: 8, 9, 24, 22, 5, 1, 4, 2, 20, 18, 21, and 10. For example, if the Moon is in the Aries Navamsha, it happens in the 8th year; if in Taurus, the 9th year; one should understand the timing for all signs in this manner. || 175 ||

<vocab>Saturn, Sun, house of enemies (6th), house of loss (12th), Moon, fourth house, Mars, malefic planet, tenth house, seventh house, cruel planet, exaltation, debilitation, ninth-division, astrology</vocab>

<summary>This page describes astrological configurations that predict the death of a child at age twenty or the premature death of the child's parents. It provides a specific chronological system for maternal mortality based on the Moon's position in the Navamsha sub-divisions.</summary>
<keywords>Arishta, maternal death, paternal death, Sun in 7th house, Navamsha timing, malefic placements, planetary exchange</keywords>

<header>Moon of the Ascendant</header>

> **When Saturn, Mars, Rahu, and the Sun are situated in the Ascendant,**
> **there is affliction and blood disorders; if all are benefics, they grant health. || 176 ||**

If <term>Saturn: "Shani"</term>, <term>Mars: "Mangala"</term>, <term>Rahu</term>, and the <term>Sun: "Surya"</term> are seated in the <term>Ascendant: "Lagna"</term> <note>the first house of the birth chart, representing the body and self</note>, then suffering and <term>blood disorders: "rakta-dosha"</term> <note>a category of illnesses in Ayurveda involving blood toxicity or imbalance</note> will persist. However, if all the planets are <term>benefics: "Saumya-graha"</term> <note>gentle planets like Jupiter, Venus, or a strong Moon</note>, then the person will remain healthy. || 176 ||

> **If even a single strong benefic is in an angular house, he is a light of the world;**
> **all defects are destroyed, and the person shall be long-lived. || 177 ||**
> **When the Sun is in an angular house, and the Moon is in the ninth-part of a friend**
> **and is viewed by Jupiter, then that person is born wealthy and endowed with knowledge. || 178 ||**

If even one <term>benefic planet: "Shubha-graha"</term> is strong and situated in an <term>angular house: "Kendra"</term> <note>the 1st, 4th, 7th, or 10th houses</note>, the person becomes a "light of the world" (famous and influential), and they will have a long life while all astrological defects are destroyed. Furthermore, if the Sun is in an angular house, and the <term>Moon: "Chandra"</term> is placed in a <term>friendly division: "Mitransha"</term> <note>a "Navamsha" or 1/9th divisional chart sign ruled by a planetary friend</note> and is aspected (viewed) by <term>Jupiter: "Brihaspati"</term>, then that person is born wealthy and possesses great wisdom. || 177-178 ||

### Now, Auspicious and Inauspicious Combinations for Women

> **If benefics are in the angular houses in rear-rising signs**
> **and malefics are in human signs in the seventh house,**
> **she shall be a queen with a vast treasury,**
> **eternally peaceful, and blessed with sons. || 1 ||**

In the horoscope of a woman, if <term>benefic planets</term> are situated in angular houses in <term>rear-rising signs: "Prishthodaya"</term> <note>signs like Aries, Taurus, or Cancer which are said to rise "back-first" on the horizon</note>, and <term>malefic planets: "Papa-graha"</term> are in <term>human signs: "Manushya-rashi"</term> <note>Gemini, Virgo, Libra, Aquarius, or the first half of Sagittarius</note> while occupying the <term>seventh house: "Kalatra-sthana"</term> <note>the house of marriage</note>, she will become a queen with a great treasury, possessing a peaceful nature and many sons. || 1 ||

> **If Mercury is in the Ascendant in its sign of exaltation,**
> **and the Priest of the Gods is in the house of gains,**
> **she becomes the wife of a king; in the company of women,**
> **she becomes famous here on earth. || 2 ||**

If <term>Mercury: "Budha"</term> is in the Ascendant while in its <term>sign of exaltation: "Tunga"</term> <note>Virgo</note>, and the <term>Priest of the Gods: "Deva-purohita"</term> <note>Jupiter</note> is located in the eleventh house <note>the house of gains and profits</note>, that woman becomes the wife of a king and achieves great fame in this world. || 2 ||

<note>original vocabulary: Lagna (Ascendant), Shani (Saturn), Mangala (Mars), Rahu (North Node), Surya (Sun), Rakta-dosha (Blood disorder), Arogya (Health), Kendra (Angular house), Dirghayu (Long-lived), Chandra (Moon), Navamsha (Ninth-part division), Brihaspati (Jupiter), Shubhashubha-yoga (Auspicious/Inauspicious combinations), Prishthodaya (Rear-rising), Kalatra (Spouse/7th house), Manushya-rashi (Human signs), Budha (Mercury), Narendra-patni (King's wife), Vanita (Woman).</note>

If exalted <term>Mercury: "Budha"</term> <note>Mercury is exalted when it is in the sign of Virgo, representing high intelligence and communication</note> is in the <term>Ascendant: "Lagna"</term> <note>the first house or rising sign</note>, and <term>Jupiter: "Brihaspati"</term> is in the eleventh house, then that woman shall be a king’s queen and become famous across the earth. || 2 ||

->Even a lone Jupiter, purified in the six-fold divisions,<-
->situated in an angular house and aspected by the Moon,<-
->makes a woman a queen, wealthy, endowed with sons,<-
->beautiful, and possessing rounded, heavy hips. || 3 ||<-

If a lone Jupiter is strong by being <term>purified in the Shadvarga: "Shadvarga-shuddha"</term> <note>a technical calculation where a planet is strong across six different divisional charts</note> and is located in an <term>angular house: "Kendra"</term> <note>the 1st, 4th, 7th, or 10th houses of the horoscope</note> and is aspected (viewed) by the <term>Moon: "Chandrama"</term>, then that woman will be very wealthy, a queen, have sons, and possess <term>stout hips: "pina-nitamba"</term> <note>in ancient Indian aesthetics, this was a traditional mark of beauty, health, and fertility</note>. || 3 ||

->With Cancer rising, the Sun in the seventh house,<-
->and aspected by Jupiter, she has a perfect body.<-
->She becomes a preeminent Vidyadhari, a queen<-
->who has conquered her enemies, with many sons and grandsons. || 4 ||<-

If <term>Cancer: "Karka"</term> is the Ascendant, the <term>Sun: "Surya"</term> is in the seventh house, and is aspected by Jupiter, she will have a perfect and excellent body, be like a <term>Vidyadhari: "supernatural being"</term> <note>referring to a class of celestial beings known for beauty and grace</note>, have many sons and grandsons, and be a chief queen. || 4 ||

->With three planets purified in the six-fold divisions, she is a queen;<-
->with four, she is a devoted wife of a high rank.<-
->With five or more, she travels in a divine chariot,<-
->and becomes the consort of the Lord of the Three Worlds. || 5 ||<-

By having three planets strong in the <term>Shadvarga: "six-fold divisions"</term>, she becomes a queen. If four or five planets are so situated, she becomes a queen who rides in a <term>divine chariot: "vimana"</term> <note>an ancient term for a celestial vehicle or palanquin</note> and is the consort of a king equal to the Lord of the Three Worlds. || 5 ||

->If the <term>Moon: "Sheetarush"</term> <note>literally "the cold-rayed one"</note> is in the eleventh house, and <term>Venus: "Bhrigu"</term><-
->is in the seventh house joined with the <term>son of the Moon: "Chandrasuta"</term> <note>Mercury</note>,<-
->and is aspected by <term>Jupiter: "Jiva"</term>, she becomes a queen here,<-
->famous on the earth and praised by everyone. || 6 ||<-

If the Moon is in the eleventh house <note>the house of gains and income</note>, and Venus joined with Mercury is in the seventh house <note>the house of partnership and marriage</note> and is aspected (viewed) by Jupiter, then she will be the most praised queen on earth. || 6 ||

->The results for women and men are the same in the birth chart,<-
->but in the seventh house particularly;<-
->good fortune and bodily appearance should be determined<-
->from both the Moon and the Ascendant. || 7 ||<-

The results of the seventh house are equal for both women and men; however, <term>good fortune: "saubhagyam"</term> and the shape of the body should be described based on the positions of the Moon and the <term>Ascendant: "Lagna"</term>. || 7 ||

->If a <term>malefic planet: "papa-graha"</term> <note>such as Mars, Saturn, or the Nodes</note> is in the Ascendant or the seventh house,<-
->the husband dies in the seventh year.<-
->If the Moon is in the sixth or eighth house,<-
->he dies in the eighth year. || 8 ||<-

If a malefic planet is in the Ascendant or the seventh house, the husband may die in the seventh year [of marriage]; and if the Moon is in the <term>sixth: "shastha"</term> or <term>eighth: "ashtama"</term> house, the husband may die in the eighth year. || 8 ||

->With Jupiter or Venus in the eighth, children die;<-
->with Mars in the eighth, she suffers miscarriages.<-
->A planet situated in the eighth house is certainly<-
->not considered auspicious for a woman. || 9 ||<-

If Jupiter and Venus are in the eighth house, her children will not survive; if <term>Mars: "Mangala"</term> is in the eighth house, the pregnancy will be lost. The underlying meaning is that any planet residing in the eighth house <note>traditionally the house of death and hidden troubles</note> at the time of a woman's birth is not considered beneficial. || 9 ||

->There will be one son who becomes a king<-
->when the <term>Sun: "Ravi"</term> is situated in the fifth house.<-
->With Mars, three sons; with Jupiter,<-
->five sons are proclaimed. || 10 ||<-

If the Sun is in the <term>fifth house: "panchama-sthana"</term> <note>the house of children and creativity</note>, there will be only one son, but he will be a king. If Mars is there, there will be three sons, and if Jupiter is there, five sons are said to be born. || 10 ||

If the <term>Moon: "Nishanatha"</term> <note>literally "Lord of the Night"</note> is located in the fifth house <note>the house of children and creativity</note>, the woman will have two daughters. If the Ascendant or the Moon is placed between two <term>malefic planets: "papa-graha"</term> <note>planets like Mars, Saturn, or Rahu that are thought to exert a challenging influence</note>, the girl born then destroys the lineage of both her father and her father-in-law. || 14 ||
If the Ascendant or the Moon is between malefic planets, the girl born at that time ruins the families of both her father and her husband's home. || 14 ||

If Mars is in the twelfth or eighth house <note>houses associated with loss and transformation</note> and a malefic planet is also situated there, and if <term>Rahu: "Simhikaputra"</term> <note>literally "the son of Simhika"</note> is in the Ascendant, the girl becomes a <term>widow: "randa"</term>. || 15 ||
If Mars is in the twelfth or eighth house and a malefic planet sits there as well, and Rahu is in the Ascendant, then that girl will be a widow. || 15 ||

One born with Venus in the seventh house <note>the house of marriage</note> brings blemish to the family. If Mars is situated in the <term>Zodiac sign of Cancer: "Karka-rashi"</term>, she behaves willfully within the houses. || 16 ||

# Moonlight of the Ascendant

If <term>Venus: "Shukra"</term> is in the seventh house <note>the house of marriage and partnerships</note>, the woman born will bring dishonor to her family; and if <term>Mars: "Mangala"</term> is situated in the <term>sign of Cancer: "Karka Rashi"</term>, she will be unfaithful. || 16 ||

->If a <term>malefic planet: "papagraha"</term> <note>planets like Mars, Saturn, or the lunar nodes</note> is in the seventh house from the <term>Ascendant: "Lagna"</term>, or the seventh house from the <term>Moon: "Chandra"</term>, it immediately destroys one of the two spouses; of this, there is no doubt. || 17 ||<-

If there is even one malefic planet in the seventh house from the Ascendant or the seventh house from the Moon, then without a doubt, one of the two—husband or wife—will die; there is no uncertainty in this. || 17 ||

->If there are planets in the Ascendant, the twelfth, the fourth, the fifth, or the seventh houses, the woman becomes subject to the husband's will, and the husband becomes subject to the woman's will. || 18 ||<-

If any planet is situated in the Ascendant, the twelfth, fourth, fifth, or seventh houses, the woman will remain under the husband's influence, and the husband will remain under the woman's influence. || 18 ||

### Now, the Results of the Seasons

->A man born in the <term>Spring season: "Vasanta Ritu"</term> is highly industrious, wise, radiant, a doer of many deeds, and familiar with the flavors and customs of many lands. || 1 ||<-

A person born in the Spring season is very hardworking, intelligent, glorious, performs many tasks, and understands the behaviors and cultures of many different countries. || 1 ||

->One born in the <term>Summer season: "Grishma"</term> begins many projects, has conquered anger, is often hungry, is passionate, tall, brave, wise, and always remains pure. || 2 ||<-

If born in the Summer season, he initiates many works, is free from anger, possesses a strong appetite, is lustful, tall, heroic, and intelligent, and always remains clean and holy. || 2 ||

->A man born in the <term>Rainy season: "Varsha Ritu"</term> will be virtuous, endowed with enjoyments, respected by kings, a master of his senses, skillful, and a speaker of truth. || 3 ||<-

### Now, the Results of the Lunar Fortnight

->Resembling the full moon in radiance, prosperous, industrious, learned in many scriptures,<-
->skillful, and wise is the man born during the <term>Waxing Moon: "Shukla Paksha"</term>. || 1 ||<-

A man born during the Waxing Moon <note>the two-week period from the New Moon to the Full Moon</note> possesses a brilliance like the full moon; he is wealthy, hardworking, knowledgeable in many sacred texts, skillful, and filled with wisdom. || 1 ||

->Harsh, foul-mouthed, foolish, hostile toward women, and abandoned by his kin,<-
->one who serves others is the man born during the <term>Waning Moon: "Krishna Paksha"</term>. || 2 ||<-

A man born during the Waning Moon <note>the two-week period from the Full Moon to the New Moon</note> is harsh, speaks unpleasantly, is ignorant, bears malice toward women, is shunned by his people, and spends his life in the service of others. || 2 ||

### Now, the Results of the Days of the Week

A man born on Sunday enjoys sweet food, is proud, prone to anger, and longs for sensual pleasure. He has an excess of bile <note>original: "pitta," one of the three bodily humors in Ayurvedic medicine</note>, and his heart is always set on the desire for wealth. || 1 ||
<gloss>If birth occurs on Sunday, one is a consumer of sweets, proud, angry, desires sexual union, has an abundance of pitta, and remains desirous of wealth. || 1 ||</gloss>

One born on Monday is an enjoyer of life’s pleasures, passionate, a scholar of the scriptures, virtuous, and proud. He has conquered his senses <note>original: "jitendriya," one who has mastery over their physical desires</note>, is highly educated, and is possessed of a good character. || 2 ||
<gloss>Born on Monday, one is an enjoyer, passionate, a knower of scriptures, virtuous, proud, a master of his senses, highly learned, and well-behaved. || 2 ||</gloss>

A man born on Tuesday is a friend to fools, wealthy, but cruel. He is a critic of the sacred revelations and traditions <note>original: "Shruti-Smriti," referring to the Vedas and subsequent legal/customary texts</note>. He is an atheist, lacks Vedic knowledge, yet lives as an enjoyer of pleasures. || 3 ||
<gloss>Born on Tuesday, one is dear to fools, wealthy, angry, a slanderer of the Shruti and Smriti, an atheist, devoid of Vedic knowledge, and an enjoyer. || 3 ||</gloss>

One born on Wednesday is engaged in the rituals of the Vedas and scriptures, is compassionate, and is "well-heard" <note>original: "bahushruta," meaning highly learned through listening to teachers</note>. He is of a fearful nature but is well-versed in the practice of <term>Yoga</term>. || 4 ||
<gloss>Born on Wednesday, one is engaged in the activities of the Vedas and scriptures, compassionate, very learned, timid, and joined with Yoga. || 4 ||</gloss>

A man born on Thursday is a knower of the Vedas and a performer of the <term>Agnihotra</term> <note>the sacred fire ritual</note>. He is blessed with sons, grandsons, and wealth; he has many followers and is a complete scholar. || 5 ||
<gloss>A knower of the Vedas, a performer of fire sacrifices, endowed with sons, grandsons, and wealth, possessed of subjects/progeny, and a perfect scholar is the man born on Thursday. || 5 ||</gloss>

A person born on Friday is purposeful, an enjoyer, and wealthy. He is brave, compassionate, and has many servants. He is also an <term>astrologer: "daivajna"</term> and a knower of the Vedas. || 6 ||
<gloss>One who achieves his aims, an enjoyer, wealthy, a brave hero, compassionate, having many servants, an astrologer, and a knower of the Vedas is the person born on Friday. || 6 ||</gloss>

## Now, Lifespan according to the Weekdays

For one born on Sunday, there is suffering <note>original: "pīḍā," referring to physical pain, illness, or astrological affliction</note> in the first month, and in the thirty-second, thirteenth, and sixth years. Such a person lives for sixty years. || 1 ||
<gloss>One born on Sunday suffers in the first month and in the 32nd, 13th, and 6th years, and lives until the age of 60. || 1 ||</gloss>

If born on a Monday, there is suffering in the eleventh or eighth month, or in the sixteenth or twenty-seventh year. The life span is eighty-four years. || 2 ||
<gloss>Born on Monday, one faces affliction in the 11th or 8th month, or the 16th or 27th year, and reaches the age of 84. || 2 ||</gloss>

If birth occurs on a Tuesday, there is suffering in the second and thirty-second years. The child remains sickly throughout life but lives for seventy-four years. || 3 ||
<gloss>One born on Tuesday faces affliction in the 2nd and 32nd years. The child is always sickly and lives for seventy-four (74) years. || 3 ||</gloss>

<header>Moonlight of the Ascendant</header>

If born on a Wednesday, there is suffering <note>original: "pīḍā," referring to physical pain or astrological affliction</note> in the eighth month and the eighth year; then, death will occur after sixty-four years have passed. || 4 ||
<gloss>If birth occurs on Wednesday, there is suffering in the eighth month and the eighth year; then death occurs when sixty-four years are completed. || 4 ||</gloss>

On a Thursday, there is suffering in the seventh month, and in the sixteenth and thirteenth years. After this, one lives for eighty-four years. || 5 ||
<gloss>If birth occurs on Thursday, there is suffering in the seventh month as well as the sixteenth and thirteenth months <note>The Hindi commentary says "months" for the latter two, though the Sanskrit verse likely implies years for the older ages</note>. Then he lives up to 84 years. || 5 ||</gloss>

For one born on a Friday, the body is free from disease. When sixty years are completed, the person certainly dies. || 6 ||
<gloss>If birth occurs on Friday, there is no suffering in the body, and when sixty years are completed, then he attains death. || 6 ||</gloss>

On a Saturday, there is suffering in the first month and the eighteenth year. Having been born with a firm body, one lives for a hundred years. || 7 ||
<gloss>If birth occurs on Saturday, there is suffering in the first month and the eighteenth year, and the body remains strong, and he lives for a hundred years. || 7 ||</gloss>

### Now, the Results of the Lunar Days <term>Tithiphalam: The predicted outcomes or character traits based on which "Tithi" (lunar day) a person is born under</term>

One born on the first lunar day <note>original: "Pratipada"</note> associates with cruel people, is devoid of wealth, causes grief to their family, and has a mind addicted to vices. || 1 ||
<gloss>One born on the first day of the lunar cycle always remains in the company of the wicked, is without wealth, brings sorrow to his own family, and is addicted to bad habits. || 1 ||</gloss>

A person born on the second lunar day <note>original: "Dvitiya"</note> is always devoted to others' wives, is devoid of truth and purity, is a thief, and lacks affection. || 2 ||

If born on the seventh <term>Saptami: The seventh lunar day</term>, one is content with little <note>original: "alpa santosh," meaning someone who finds peace easily without needing much</note>, possesses a glowing aura, is handsome due to his good fortune, has sons, and is full of riches. || 7 ||

<meta>This page continues the "Tithiphalam" (Results of the Lunar Days) section from page 50. <header>With Vernacular Commentary</header>

**Wealthy, righteous, brave, a keeper of his word;**
**Respected by the king and famous, when born on the fourteenth. || 14 ||**
If one is born on the fourteenth lunar day <term>Chaturdashi: The fourteenth day of the lunar fortnight, just before the Full or New Moon</term>, they will be possessed of Lakshmi <note>meaning they will be wealthy</note>, virtuous, a brave warrior, one who fulfills their promises, respected by royalty, and highly renowned. || 14 ||

**Prosperous, intelligent, and fond of grand feasts;**
**Radiant, detached from the wives of others, is one born on the Full Moon. || 15 ||**
One born on the Full Moon <term>Purnima: The night of the full moon</term> is wealthy, endowed with intellect, great, has a longing for fine food, is radiant <note>original: "tejasvi"; possessing spiritual or physical luster</note>, and refrains from consorting with other women <note>The Hindi commentary suggests "enjoying other women," but the Sanskrit "viratah" explicitly means "detached" or "abstaining from."</note>. || 15 ||

**Persistent in undertakings, a hater of others, crooked, foolish, and brave;**
**A keeper of secrets and truly wise is the man born on the New Moon. || 16 ||**
One born on the New Moon <term>Amavasya: The night of the new moon</term> is one who persists in the work they start, is hostile toward others, cunning, foolish, courageous, a counselor of secrets, and possesses right knowledge. || 16 ||

**In an "unpierced" lunar day or one "pierced" by a gentle planet, the result is auspicious;**
**But in a day "pierced" by a malefic planet, the result is surely unfavorable. || 17 ||**
If one is born on a day that is "unpierced" <term>Nirviddha: A lunar day that does not overlap with an inauspicious time or planet</term> or is "pierced" <term>Viddha: An astrological term where one lunar day is influenced by the preceding or succeeding one</term> by a benefic planet, the result is good. However, if the birth occurs on a day "pierced" by a malefic planet <note>original: "papaviddha"</note>, know for certain that the result will be undesirable or harmful. || 17 ||

### Now, the Results of the Nanda and Other Lunar Day Groups

**A man born on a Nanda lunar day is very proud and learned;**
**Devoted to the worship of the gods, wise, and affectionate toward loved ones. || 1 ||**
A child born on a "Nanda" lunar day <term>Nanda: A group of lunar days comprising the 1st, 6th, and 11th days of the fortnight</term> is highly self-respecting, a scholar <note>original: "pandit"</note>, diligent in the devotion of deities, wise, and a benefactor to his dear ones. || 1 ||

**On a Bhadra day, one is respected by kin, a servant of kings, and wealthy;**
**Fearful of the cycle of worldly existence and possessed of a spiritual mind. || 2 ||**
A person born on a "Bhadra" lunar day <term>Bhadra: A group of lunar days comprising the 2nd, 7th, and 12th days of the fortnight</term> is honored by their relatives, serves the royalty, is endowed with wealth, fears the cycle of rebirth <note>original: "samsara"</note>, and has an intellect focused on the highest truth. || 2 ||

A person born in **Krittika** <term>Krittika: The Pleiades; often associated with fire and sharp energy</term> is a miser, a sinner, gluttonous, always distressed, and a doer of evil deeds. || 3 ||

->**Born in Ashlesha, a man performs the eight [bad] deeds. || 9 ||**<-

<note>The Sanskrit verse for Ashlesha (9) lists "sarvabhakṣaḥ," literally "one who eats everything," implying a lack of discrimination or great greed.</note>

--- Page 58 ---
<meta>This page continues the "Nakshatraphalam" (Results of the Birth Stars) section from the Lagnachandrika, a classical text on natal astrology. Having previously covered the results of birth in the early lunar mansions (Krittika through Punarvasu), this page details the characteristics of individuals born under the stars Ashlesha through Chitra.</meta>

<page-num>54</page-num>
<header>Lagnachandrika</header>

One born in the **Ashlesha** <term>Ashlesha: The "Embracer" or "Clinging Star," symbolized by a coiled serpent and associated with intense, sometimes manipulative or secretive energy</term> nakshatra eats everything <note>This suggests a lack of dietary discipline or a gluttonous nature</note>, possesses a cruel nature <note>original: "krutanta," which can mean one who acts like the god of death</note>, is ungrateful, a deceiver, wicked, and a performer of corrupt deeds. || 9 ||

> **Welfare in the first quarter; loss of wealth in the second;**
> **Pain to the mother in the third; and in the fourth of Ashlesha, the father. || 10 ||**

If born in the first quarter of the Ashlesha nakshatra, there is welfare; if in the second quarter, there is loss of wealth; if in the third quarter, it causes pain to the mother; and if born in the fourth quarter, it destroys the father <note>In traditional Indian astrology, certain quarters of "sharp" stars like Ashlesha were thought to bring misfortune to specific family members</note>. || 10 ||

> **Having many servants, wealthy, an enjoyer, devoted to the father, very industrious;**
> **An army chief and servant to kings is the man born in Magha. || 11 ||**

One born in the **Magha** <term>Magha: "The Mighty One," located in the constellation of Leo and symbolized by a royal throne; it is governed by the Pitris (Ancestors)</term> nakshatra has many servants, is wealthy, an enjoyer of life’s pleasures, devoted to their father, highly industrious, a commander of armies, and serves royalty. || 11 ||

> **Endowed with knowledge, cattle, and wealth; serious and dear to women;**
> **Born in Purva Phalguni, one is happy and honored by scholars. || 12 ||**

If born in **Purva Phalguni** <term>Purva Phalguni: "The Former Reddish One," associated with the god Bhaga (fortune) and representing relaxation, creative arts, and social ease</term>, one is endowed with learning and wealth in the form of cattle, is serious, beloved by women, happy, and respected by wise scholars. || 12 ||

> **A giver, brave, soft-spoken, a scholar of the meaning of archery;**
> **Born in Uttara Phalguni, a great warrior beloved by the people. || 13 ||**

One born in **Uttara Phalguni** <term>Uttara Phalguni: "The Latter Reddish One," associated with the sun and representing social duty, companionship, and contracts</term> is a donor, a hero, gentle-spoken, an expert in the science of archery <note>original: "dhanurveda," the ancient Indian science of weaponry and warfare</note>, a great warrior, and beloved by all people. || 13 ||

> **Speaking untruths, wicked, a wine-drinker, abandoned by kin;**
> **Born in Hasta, one becomes a thief and an adulterer. || 14 ||**

The person born in the **Hasta** <term>Hasta: "The Hand," symbolized by a fist or hand, representing skill, craftsmanship, or the power to grasp and control</term> nakshatra is a liar, wicked, a drinker of intoxicants, devoid of relatives, a thief, and one who consorts with the wives of others. || 14 ||

> **Endowed with sons and a wife, contented, possessing wealth and grain;**
> **A devotee of gods and Brahmins is the man born in Chitra. || 15 ||**

A person born in **Chitra** <term>Chitra: "The Bright One" or "The Multi-colored One," symbolized by a shining jewel and associated with the celestial architect Vishvakarma</term> is endowed with sons and a wife, is satisfied, possesses wealth and food-grains, and is a devotee of the gods and the learned class. || 15 ||

It ranges from the harsh warnings regarding Ashlesha to the more auspicious and royal descriptions of Magha and Chitra.</summary>

--- Page 59 ---
<meta>This page continues the "Nakshatraphalam" (Results of Birth Stars) from the previous page. Having just finished the description of the Chitra nakshatra, the text proceeds to detail the temperaments and fates of those born under Swati, Vishakha, Anuradha, Jyeshtha, and Mula.</meta>

<page-num>55</page-num>
<header>With Language Commentary</header>

A son born in **Chitra** <term>Chitra: "The Bright" or "The Brilliant," often associated with craftsmanship, beauty, and the star Spica</term> is united with a wife, is cheerful, endowed with wealth and grain, and is a devotee of the gods and Brahmins. || 15 ||

> **A scholar, righteous, possessing little wealth,**
> **Frugal, beloved of women,**
> **Happy and a devotee of his own deity is the man born in Swati. || 16 ||**

A man born in the **Swati** <term>Swati: "The Independent One" or "The Sword," associated with the wind god Vayu and characterized by independence and movement</term> nakshatra is a scholar, religious, possesses modest wealth, is frugal <note>original: "kripanah," which can mean miserly or simply very cautious with spending</note>, is dear to women, happy, and a devotee of his chosen personal deity. || 16 ||

> **Extremely greedy, very sinful, harsh, and fond of quarrels;**
> **The man born in Vishakha is one who delights in the company of prostitutes. || 17 ||**

A man born in the **Vishakha** <term>Vishakha: "The Forked" or "Entered into Branches," symbolized by a decorated arch or a potter's wheel; it is associated with focused ambition</term> nakshatra is extremely greedy, sinful, harsh, fond of picking fights, and indulges in the company of prostitutes. || 17 ||

> **Industrious, a traveler, always energetic in the service of kinsmen;**
> **A person born in Anuradha is always born with a joyful heart. || 18 ||**

One born in the **Anuradha** <term>Anuradha: "Following Radha" or "Subsequent Success," associated with friendship, cooperation, and the deity Mitra</term> nakshatra is industrious, lives in foreign lands <note>original: "pravasi," meaning one who dwells away from home</note>, is always ready to assist his brothers and relatives, and is constantly joyful. || 18 ||

> **Having many friends, a leader, a seeker of knowledge, and clever;**
> **One born in Jyeshtha is devoted to dharma and is honored by the Shudras. || 19 ||**

A man born in the **Jyeshtha** <term>Jyeshtha: "The Eldest" or "The Most Excellent," associated with the king of gods, Indra; it signifies seniority and protective power</term> nakshatra has many friends, is a prominent person, possesses a desire for learning, is wise, intent on performing his religious duties, and is honored by the Shudra <note>The laborer or service class in the traditional four-fold social structure</note> people. || 19 ||

> **A steady enjoyer, respectable, wealthy, and happy;**
> **One is such if born in the third or fourth quarter of Mula;**
> **One should abandon the child in the first half of Mula. || 20 ||**

A man born in the third or fourth quarter <note>Each Nakshatra is divided into four quarters or "Padas"</note> of the **Mula** <term>Mula: "The Root," located at the galactic center and associated with Nirriti, the goddess of destruction; it represents getting to the bottom of things</term> nakshatra is a steady enjoyer of pleasures, is honorable, wealthy, and happy. However, a child born in the first half of the Mula nakshatra should be abandoned <note>This reflects an ancient, harsh astrological belief that births in the transition point or "root" of this star were extremely inauspicious for the family, often requiring specific pacification rituals or, in extreme historical contexts, symbolic abandonment</note>. || 20 ||

<summary>This page lists the personality traits and social standing of individuals born under the lunar mansions from Chitra to Mula, including specific warnings about the timing of birth within the Mula nakshatra.</summary>
<keywords>Chitra, Swati, Vishakha, Anuradha, Jyeshtha, Mula, Nakshatra, Vedic Astrology, character traits, lunar mansions</keywords>

<page-num>56</page-num>
<header>Lagnachandrika (The Moonlight of the Ascendant)</header>

### Lagnachandrika

**If born in the first quarter, there is suffering for the father; in the second quarter of Mula, for the mother. In the third, there is loss of wealth, and in the fourth, there is happiness in every way. || 21 ||**

A person born in the first quarter <term>Pada: One of the four divisions or "steps" of a Nakshatra, each corresponding to 3 degrees and 20 minutes of the zodiac</term> of the **Mula** <term>Mula: "The Root," located at the galactic center and associated with the goddess Nirriti (destruction/transformation)</term> nakshatra brings distress to the father; in the second quarter, to the mother; in the third quarter, it causes the loss of riches; and birth in the fourth quarter brings all types of happiness and prosperity. || 21 ||

**One who is helpful simply by being seen, fortunate, and a favorite of the people; the man born in Purvashadha is clever in all matters. || 22 ||**

One born in the **Purvashadha** <term>Purvashadha: "The Former Invincible One," associated with the element of water and the deity Apah</term> nakshatra is a benefactor who helps others just by his presence. He is lucky, beloved by all, and highly skilled and discerning in all tasks and objectives. || 22 ||

**With many friends, a large physique, religious, humble, and happy; one born in Uttarashadha is a hero and victorious in battle. || 23 ||**

The person born in **Uttarashadha** <term>Uttarashadha: "The Latter Invincible One," associated with the Vishvadevas (Universal Gods) and representing permanent victory</term> possesses a wide circle of friends and a large, robust body. He is righteous, modest, and leads a joyful life. Such a person is a courageous warrior who achieves victory in conflict <note>original: "rane," meaning in battle or any field of competition</note>. || 23 ||

**Grateful, humble, charitable, and always free from disease; the man born in Shravana is wealthy and has many children. || 24 ||**

One born in the **Shravana** <term>Shravana: "Hearing" or "The Star of Learning," symbolized by an ear or three footprints, and associated with the god Vishnu</term> nakshatra is characterized by gratitude and humility. He is a donor of charity, enjoys constant good health, is blessed with fortune, and has a large family. || 24 ||

**A lover of song, respected by his kinsmen, and adorned with gold and jewels; a man born in Dhanishtha becomes a leader of a hundred. || 25 ||**

The person born in **Dhanishtha** <term>Dhanishtha: "The Richest One" or "The Most Famous," associated with the eight Vasus (elemental gods) and music</term> has a great love for music and songs. He is highly esteemed by his relatives and possesses fine ornaments of gold and gems. Such a person is a natural leader, capable of commanding a hundred people alone <note>original: "shatapati," a title for a commander or headman of a hundred villages or soldiers</note>. || 25 ||

**A miser who is full of wealth, a servant of others' wives; the man in Shatatara is devoted to traveling to foreign lands. || 26 ||**

A person born in **Shatabhisha** <term>Shatabhisha: Also called Shatatara, "The Hundred Physicians" or "The Hundred Stars," associated with the god Varuna and healing</term> is a miser <note>original: "kripanah," which can imply a person who is extremely frugal or stingy despite having great wealth</note>, yet he is very wealthy. He may be prone to seeking the company of other men's wives and is constantly inclined toward traveling or living in foreign countries. || 26 ||

**An eloquent speaker, happy, blessed with children, a heavy sleeper, and unproductive; the man born in Purva Bhadrapada is one who experiences sorrow. || 27 ||**

A person born under the **Purva Bhadrapada** <term>Purva Bhadrapada: "The Former Blessed Feet," a nakshatra associated with fire and the funeral cot, representing transformation and intense focus</term> nakshatra is a skilled conversationalist and enjoys happiness and the company of his children. However, he is prone to excessive sleep, can be idle or aimless at times, and is subject to bouts of grief. || 27 ||

**Fair-complexioned, knowledgeable in all religious duties, a destroyer of enemies, and lowly; one born in the Uttara Bhadra nakshatra is courageous and persistent. || 28 ||**

One born under the **Uttara Bhadrapada** <term>Uttara Bhadrapada: "The Latter Blessed Feet," associated with the deity Ahirbudhnya (the serpent of the depths) and symbolizing wisdom and stability</term> nakshatra has a fair complexion and a deep understanding of all religious laws <note>original: "dharma"</note>. He is a subduer of his foes, yet may possess some base or insignificant traits, and is known for being headstrong and brave. || 28 ||

**With perfect limbs, pure, skillful, virtuous, heroic, and wise; one born in Revati is adorned with wealth and grain in this world. || 29 ||**

A person born in the **Revati** <term>Revati: "The Wealthy One," the final nakshatra of the zodiac, symbolizing journeys and the completion of cycles</term> nakshatra possesses an excellent and well-formed physique. He is pure in heart, clever, a man of saintly character, courageous, and a learned scholar. He is well-adorned with riches and agricultural abundance. || 29 ||

**If the birth occurs in a nakshatra free from obstruction and joined by benefic planets, the results are auspicious. The results are the opposite for one born in a star that is obstructed or afflicted by cruel planets. || 30 ||**

If a person is born when the nakshatra is free of **Vedha** <term>Vedha: An astrological "piercing" or obstruction where the presence of a planet in one star cancels or alters the effects of another star</term> or is associated with auspicious <note>original: "saumya" (gentle/benefic)</note> planets, then the results are good. If the nakshatra is afflicted by obstruction or "cruel" <term>Malefic: "Krura" planets like Mars or Saturn that are believed to bring harsh or challenging influences</term> planets, the results will be unfavorable. || 30 ||

## Now, the Results of the Yogas

**A man born in Vishkumbha is handsome and virtuous; he is adorned with many ornaments and is a master of great intelligence. || 1 ||**

A person born in the **Vishkumbha** <term>Yoga: In this context, one of the 27 Nitya Yogas, which are daily mathematical angles between the Sun and Moon. Vishkumbha is the first of these, meaning "the Pot of Poison," though its effects here are described as positive for the individual</term> yoga is beautiful in appearance, possesses many good qualities, wears various types of jewelry, and is a highly intelligent expert or scholar. || 1 ||

--- Page 62 ---
<meta>This page transitions from the descriptions of the twenty-seven Nakshatras (lunar mansions) to the Nitya Yogas. In Vedic astrology, a "Yoga" is a specific angular relationship between the Sun and the Moon. There are twenty-seven such Yogas, and each is believed to impart distinct personality traits and life outcomes to those born during their duration. This page covers the second through seventh Yogas: Priti, Ayushman, Saubhagya, Shobhana, Atiganda, and Sukarma.</meta>

<page-num>५८</page-num>
<header>The Moonlight of the Ascendant</header>

**One born in the Priti Yoga becomes a favorite of women, a knower of the fundamental truths, highly enthusiastic, self-interested, and constantly making an effort. || 2 ||**

If one is born during the **Priti** <term>Priti: Literally meaning "love" or "affection"; the second of the twenty-seven astrological yogas</term> Yoga, they will be dear to women, a knower of the essential nature of reality <note>original: "tattvajña"</note>, possess great spirit, and be daily engaged in pursuing their own interests. || 2 ||

**One born in the name of the Ayushman Yoga is honorable, wealthy, a poet, long-lived, endowed with virtuous strength, and undefeated in battle. || 3 ||**

One born in the **Ayushman** <term>Ayushman: Meaning "long-lived" or "possessing vitality"</term> Yoga is respected, wealthy, a poet, possesses a long life, is full of vital energy <note>original: "sattva"</note>, and is a brave warrior who remains undefeated in war. || 3 ||

**One born in the Saubhagya Yoga becomes a minister to the king, skillful in all tasks, and a favorite of women. || 4 ||**

The person born in the **Saubhagya** <term>Saubhagya: Meaning "good fortune" or "conjugal felicity"</term> Yoga becomes an advisor or minister to a ruler, is expert in all types of work, and is beloved by women. || 4 ||

**A child born in Shobhana Yoga is beautiful, possesses many children and wives, is eager in all tasks, and is always enthusiastic on the battlefield. || 5 ||**

A child born in the **Shobhana** <term>Shobhana: Meaning "brilliant," "splendid," or "virtuous"</term> Yoga is handsome, has many sons and a wife, is quick to act in all matters, and shows great zeal in the arena of battle. || 5 ||

**One born in Atiganda Yoga becomes a destroyer of his mother; one born at the very end of the Ganda points is declared a destroyer of the entire lineage. || 6 ||**

One born in the **Atiganda** <term>Atiganda: Meaning "a great knot" or "severe obstacle"; considered an inauspicious yoga</term> Yoga may bring harm to his mother, and if born during the final moments <note>original: "ghatiyas," a traditional unit of time equal to 24 minutes</note> of this Yoga, he is said to bring ruin to his entire family line. || 6 ||

**One born in Sukarma Yoga becomes a man of good deeds, loved by all, of good character, passionate, an enjoyer of life, and possesses great virtues. || 7 ||**

One born in the **Sukarma** <term>Sukarma: Meaning "good actions" or "virtuous deeds"</term> Yoga performs beautiful and righteous deeds; he is pleasing to everyone, possesses a fine character, is affectionate, enjoys life's pleasures, and is exceptionally virtuous. || 7 ||

<summary>This page describes the character traits and destinies associated with six of the twenty-seven Nitya Yogas, ranging from the auspicious and wealthy Ayushman and Saubhagya Yogas to the traditionally maligned Atiganda Yoga.</summary>

<header>With Language Commentary</header>

**In the Dhriti Yoga, one is patient, endowed with fame, nourishment, and wealth; they become fortunate, virtuous, learned, and handsome. || 8 ||**

One born in the **Dhriti** <term>Dhriti: Literally "patience" or "fortitude"; the eighth astrological yoga</term> Yoga is characterized by resolution and patience. They possess fame, physical well-being <note>original: "pushti"</note>, and riches. Such a person is lucky, full of good qualities, well-educated, and possesses a pleasing physical appearance. || 8 ||

**In Shula, one is afflicted by sharp pains, yet is religious and a master of the scriptures. Such a person is always skilled in the pursuit of knowledge and a performer of sacrifices. || 9 ||**

If one is born in the **Shula** <term>Shula: Meaning "a spear" or "sharp pain"; often associated with colic or piercing physical ailments</term> Yoga, they may suffer from "shula" <note>likely referring to chronic sharp pains or digestive disorders</note>. Despite this, they are naturally inclined toward religion and are experts in the sacred texts. They are proficient in their studies and dedicated to the performance of Vedic sacrifices <note>original: "yajva"</note>. || 9 ||

**In Ganda, one is afflicted by glandular ailments, faces much distress, and has a large head. They have a short stature, are very stout, enjoy many pleasures, and are firm in their vows. || 10 ||**

One born in the **Ganda** <term>Ganda: Meaning a "knot," "joint," or "obstacle"</term> Yoga suffers from ailments related to the throat or glands <note>original: "gaṇḍamālā"; historically referring to inflammation of the lymph nodes or goiter</note>. Such a person faces many hardships in life and is physically characterized by a large head, a short body, and a very heavy or stout build. However, they are great enjoyers of life and remain steadfast in their spiritual or personal commitments. || 10 ||

**In the Vriddhi Yoga, one has a long life and a sight pleasing to all; they are handsome, have many sons, are artistic, and have a virtuous wife. || 11 ||**

One born in the **Vriddhi** <term>Vriddhi: Meaning "growth," "increase," or "prosperity"</term> Yoga is blessed with longevity. They have a charming presence that everyone enjoys looking at, a beautiful form, and are blessed with many children. They are skilled in the arts <note>original: "kalāvān"</note> and possess a good and faithful spouse. || 11 ||

**In the Dhruva Yoga, a man is extremely powerful and always steady in his work. He becomes wealthy, a great enjoyer of pleasures, and is also full of vital energy. || 12 ||**

One born in the **Dhruva** <term>Dhruva: Meaning "fixed," "constant," or "the North Star"</term> Yoga is exceptionally capable and consistent in their endeavors. Such a person is wealthy, indulges greatly in life's comforts, and possesses a strong, courageous spirit <note>original: "sattvavān"</note>. || 12 ||

**One born in the Vyaghata Yoga is all-knowing and honored by all; they are a performer of all tasks and famous in the world for their deeds. || 13 ||**

One born in the **Vyaghata** <term>Vyaghata: Literally "beating" or "striking," but here referring to the thirteenth yoga</term> Yoga is omniscient or highly knowledgeable and is respected by everyone. They are industrious, capable of completing any task, and gain worldwide renown for their various accomplishments. || 13 ||

> **One born in Harshana is a great enjoyer of life in this world, dear to the king, joyful, always endowed with wealth, and an expert in the Vedas and scriptures. || 14 ||**

If one is born in the **Harshana** <term>Harshana: Meaning "thrilling" or "causing joy"</term> Yoga, they become a great enjoyer of pleasures in the world, a favorite of the ruler <note>original: "nripapriya"</note>, cheerful, always possessed of riches, and a scholar of the sacred Vedas and various sciences. || 14 ||

> **In the Vajra Yoga, one has a fist like a thunderbolt, is a master of all sciences, is endowed with wealth and grain, and is a man of thunderbolt-like valor. || 15 ||**

If one is born in the **Vajra** <term>Vajra: Meaning "thunderbolt" or "diamond"; symbolizing indestructibility and power</term> Yoga, they possess a grip as strong as a thunderbolt <note>original: "vajramushti"</note>, are proficient in every field of knowledge, possess an abundance of money and food, and are as courageous as a thunderbolt. || 15 ||

> **One born in the Siddhi Yoga becomes endowed with all types of success. Such a person is a donor, an enjoyer, happy, and handsome, yet also prone to sorrow and illness. || 16 ||**

If one is born in the **Siddhi** <term>Siddhi: Meaning "perfection," "success," or "accomplishment"</term> Yoga, they achieve success in all their works. They are generous, capable of enjoying life’s pleasures, happy, and physically attractive <note>original: "kanta"</note>, though they may also experience periods of grief and physical ailment. || 16 ||

> **Born in Vyatipata, one lives through great hardship. If they survive through the influence of good fortune, they become the most excellent among men. || 17 ||**

If one is born in **Vyatipata** <term>Vyatipata: Meaning "calamity" or "great fall"; considered a very powerful and often difficult astrological combination</term>, they live a life of immense struggle. However, if they manage to survive by the grace of destiny, they rise to become the most distinguished of people. || 17 ||

> **In the Variyan Yoga, a man becomes most excellent, knowledgeable in crafts, poetry, and the arts, and is an expert in song, dance, and other such skills. || 18 ||**

If one is born in the **Variyan** <term>Variyan: Meaning "most excellent" or "preferable"</term> Yoga, they become highly distinguished and are well-versed in crafts <note>original: "shilpa"</note>, poetry, and various arts, as well as being skilled in music and dancing. || 18 ||

> **A man born in Parigha brings progress to his own lineage; he is an expert in the scriptures, a poet, eloquent, a donor, an enjoyer, and speaks sweet words. || 19 ||**

### Now, the Results of the Karanas
<note>Karana: An astrological division of a lunar day (Tithi); there are 11 Karanas in total, with two occurring in each Tithi.</note>

->**One born in the Taitila Karana is endowed with the quality of good fortune, affectionate toward all people, and possesses wonderful houses. || 4 ||**<-

If born in the **Taitila** <term>Taitila: One of the eleven Karanas (astrological lunar half-days); generally considered auspicious</term> Karana, a person is blessed with the virtue of good luck, maintains affectionate relations with everyone, and acquires unique or remarkable dwellings. || 4 ||

->**In the one named Gara, one is a farmer and devoted to household duties. Whatever object is desired is obtained here through great effort. || 5 ||**<-

If born in the **Gara** <term>Gara: The fourth of the mobile Karanas; associated with hard work and the earth</term> Karana, one becomes a practitioner of agriculture, is diligent in managing household affairs, and attains whatever he desires by putting in significant effort. || 5 ||

->**One born in the Vanija Karana lives specifically by trade. He obtains his desires in the world through travels to other countries. || 6 ||**<-

If born in the **Vanija** <term>Vanija: Meaning 'merchant'; the fifth mobile Karana</term> Karana, one earns a livelihood through commerce and achieves his goals in the world by traveling to and from foreign lands. || 6 ||

->**One born in the Karana named Vishti is inclined toward starting inauspicious deeds, skilled in works involving poison, and always intent on harming others. || 7 ||**<-

If born in the **Vishti** <term>Vishti: Also known as Bhadra; often considered an inauspicious period in electional astrology</term> Karana, he initiates harmful or ill-fated works, is skilled in matters involving poisons <note>original: "visha-karyeshu," which can refer to medicine, toxicology, or destructive chemistry</note>, and is constantly engaged in causing harm to others. || 7 ||

->**One whose birth was in Shakuni performs nourishing and strengthening actions; he is skilled in medicines and becomes a physician by profession. || 8 ||**<-

Whoever is born in the **Shakuni** <term>Shakuni: The eighth Karana and the first of the four "fixed" Karanas</term> Karana performs restorative or strengthening <note>original: "paushtika," relating to nutrition or prosperity rituals</note> activities, is clever with medicinal herbs, and makes a living as a physician. || 8 ||

->**And in the Chatushpada Karana, [one is] always devoted to the gods and the twice-born. [He engages in] cattle-work, is a master of cattle in the world, and a veterinarian of quadrupeds. || 9 ||**<-

<note>The vernacular commentary for verse 9 is missing or truncated in the original manuscript.</note>

<vocab>Karana (astrological half-day), Jyotisha (Astrology), Taitila, Gara, Vanija, Vishti, Shakuni, Chatushpada (four-legged), Bhashatika (language commentary)</vocab>

These results range from auspicious traits like luck and skill in medicine to inauspicious traits such as malice or involvement with poisons.</summary>

<page-num>६४</page-num>
<header>Lagnachandrika</header>

If born in the **Chatushpada** <term>Chatushpada: Literally "four-footed"; the ninth Karana and the second of the four "fixed" Karanas</term> Karana, he always maintains devotion toward the deities and Brahmins, works with cattle in the world, is an owner of cows, and serves as a healer for four-footed animals. || 9 ||

**One born in the Karana named Naga is a lover of stationary objects, performs harsh deeds, is unfortunate, and has restless eyes. || 10 ||**

If born in the **Naga** <term>Naga: The tenth Karana, associated with the serpent; the third of the fixed Karanas</term> Karana, he maintains an affection for stationary things <note>original: "sthavara," implying immovable property like land or trees</note>, performs cruel or difficult tasks, is unlucky, and possesses wandering eyes. || 10 ||

**A man born in the Kinstughna Karana is devoted to auspicious deeds; he always attains satisfaction, nourishment, auspiciousness, and success. || 11 ||**

If born in the **Kinstughna** <term>Kinstughna: The eleventh Karana and the last of the four fixed Karanas; it is generally considered favorable</term> Karana, he remains constantly engaged in virtuous actions and always gains contentment, prosperity, auspiciousness, and the fulfillment of his goals. || 11 ||

### Now, the Results of the Zodiac Signs Beginning with Aries

**A person born in the sign of Aries has restless eyes, is often ill, and is determined in duty and wealth. He has thick thighs, is grateful, courageous, and honored by kings. || 1 ||**
**He brings joy to the hearts of women, is a donor, yet is afraid even of water. He performs fierce deeds but is gentle in the end. || 2 ||**

If one’s birth occurs when the Moon is in the sign of **Aries** <term>Mesha: The first sign of the zodiac, represented by the Ram and ruled by the planet Mars</term>, he will have restless eyes, be prone to illness, be resolute in matters of religion <note>original: "Dharma"</note> and wealth <note>original: "Artha"</note>, have stout thighs, be appreciative of favors <note>original: "krutagya," one who acknowledges what has been done for them</note>, and be strong and honored by rulers. He is a delight to the hearts of women, a generous giver, possesses a fear of water, performs intense or harsh actions, but ultimately possesses a mild or gentle nature. || 1–2 ||

**An enjoyer, a donor, pure, capable, very proud, and very strong; one born in Taurus is wealthy, luxurious, radiant, and a good friend. || 3 ||**

One born in **Taurus** <term>Vrisha: The second sign of the zodiac, represented by the Bull and ruled by the planet Venus</term> becomes a person who enjoys life's pleasures, is generous, pure in conduct, skillful, possesses great pride and strength, is wealthy, fond of luxury, charismatic, and a loyal friend. || 3 ||
